===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1240-1300
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1240 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1241 |     // unknown
1242 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1243 |       return true;
1244 |     }
1245 |     // all type
1246 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1247 |       return true;
1248 |     }
1249 |     // equality
1250 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1251 |       return true;
1252 |     }
1253 |     // unions
1254 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1255 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1256 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1257 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1258 |           return true;
1259 |         }
1260 |       }
1261 |       return false;
1262 |     }
1263 | 
1264 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1265 |     // are not equivalent.
1266 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1267 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1268 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1269 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1270 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1271 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1272 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1273 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1274 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1275 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1276 | 
1277 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1278 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1279 |     } else {
1280 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1281 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1282 |     }
1283 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1284 |       return false;
1285 |     }
1286 | 
1287 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1288 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1289 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1290 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1291 |               thatType);
1292 |     }
1293 | 
1294 |     // proxy types
1295 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1296 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1297 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1298 |     }
1299 |     return false;
1300 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.describeFunctionBind(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 388-427
Documentation:

Method code:
388 |   @Override
389 |   public Bind describeFunctionBind(Node n, boolean useTypeInfo) {
390 |     Bind result = super.describeFunctionBind(n, useTypeInfo);
391 |     if (result != null) {
392 |       return result;
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     if (!n.isCall()) {
396 |       return null;
397 |     }
398 | 
399 |     Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
400 |     String name = callTarget.getQualifiedName();
401 |     if (name != null) {
402 |       if (name.equals("goog.bind")
403 |           || name.equals("goog$bind")) {
404 |         // goog.bind(fn, self, args...);
405 |         Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
406 |         if (fn == null) {
407 |           return null;
408 |         }
409 |         Node thisValue = safeNext(fn);
410 |         Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
411 |         return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
412 |       }
413 | 
414 |       if (name.equals("goog.partial") || name.equals("goog$partial")) {
415 |         // goog.partial(fn, args...);
416 |         Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
417 |         if (fn == null) {
418 |           return null;
419 |         }
420 |         Node thisValue = null;
421 |         Node parameters = safeNext(fn);
422 |         return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     return null;
427 |   }

===== M0016 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 177-211
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
177 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
178 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
179 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
180 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
181 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
182 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
183 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
184 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
185 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
186 |             !thisParamType.checkEquivalenceHelper(
187 |                 otherParamType, eqMethod)) {
188 |           return false;
189 |         }
190 |       } else {
191 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
192 |           return false;
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 | 
196 |       // Check var_args/optionality
197 |       if (thisParam.isOptionalArg() != otherParam.isOptionalArg()) {
198 |         return false;
199 |       }
200 | 
201 |       if (thisParam.isVarArgs() != otherParam.isVarArgs()) {
202 |         return false;
203 |       }
204 | 
205 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
206 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
207 |     }
208 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
209 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
210 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
211 |   }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 495-876
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
495 |   @Override
496 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
497 |     JSType childType;
498 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
499 |     Node left, right;
500 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
501 |     boolean typeable = true;
502 | 
503 |     switch (n.getType()) {
504 |       case Token.CAST:
505 |         Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
506 |         JSType exprType = getJSType(expr);
507 |         JSType castType = getJSType(n);
508 | 
509 |         // TODO(johnlenz): determine if we can limit object literals in some
510 |         // way.
511 |         if (!expr.isObjectLit()) {
512 |           validator.expectCanCast(t, n, castType, exprType);
513 |         }
514 |         ensureTyped(t, n, castType);
515 | 
516 |         if (castType.isSubtype(exprType) || expr.isObjectLit()) {
517 |           expr.setJSType(castType);
518 |         }
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.NAME:
522 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
526 |         typeable = false;
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.COMMA:
530 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.TRUE:
534 |       case Token.FALSE:
535 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
536 |         break;
537 | 
538 |       case Token.THIS:
539 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.NULL:
543 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NUMBER:
547 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.STRING:
551 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
555 |         typeable = false;
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
559 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
560 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
564 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
565 |         break;
566 | 
567 |       case Token.REGEXP:
568 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.GETPROP:
572 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
573 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
574 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.GETELEM:
578 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
579 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
580 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
581 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
582 |         typeable = false;
583 |         break;
584 | 
585 |       case Token.VAR:
586 |         visitVar(t, n);
587 |         typeable = false;
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.NEW:
591 |         visitNew(t, n);
592 |         break;
593 | 
594 |       case Token.CALL:
595 |         visitCall(t, n);
596 |         typeable = !parent.isExprResult();
597 |         break;
598 | 
599 |       case Token.RETURN:
600 |         visitReturn(t, n);
601 |         typeable = false;
602 |         break;
603 | 
604 |       case Token.DEC:
605 |       case Token.INC:
606 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
607 |         checkPropCreation(t, left);
608 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
609 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
610 |         break;
611 | 
612 |       case Token.NOT:
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.VOID:
617 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
618 |         break;
619 | 
620 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
621 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
622 |         break;
623 | 
624 |       case Token.BITNOT:
625 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
626 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
627 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
628 |               childType.toString());
629 |         }
630 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.POS:
634 |       case Token.NEG:
635 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
636 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
637 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
638 |         break;
639 | 
640 |       case Token.EQ:
641 |       case Token.NE:
642 |       case Token.SHEQ:
643 |       case Token.SHNE: {
644 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
645 |         right = n.getLastChild();
646 | 
647 |         if (left.isTypeOf()) {
648 |           if (right.isString()) {
649 |             checkTypeofString(t, right, right.getString());
650 |           }
651 |         } else if (right.isTypeOf() && left.isString()) {
652 |           checkTypeofString(t, left, left.getString());
653 |         }
654 | 
655 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
656 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
657 | 
658 |         // We do not want to warn about explicit comparisons to VOID. People
659 |         // often do this if they think their type annotations screwed up.
660 |         //
661 |         // We do want to warn about cases where people compare things like
662 |         // (Array|null) == (Function|null)
663 |         // because it probably means they screwed up.
664 |         //
665 |         // This heuristic here is not perfect, but should catch cases we
666 |         // care about without too many false negatives.
667 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
668 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
669 | 
670 |         TernaryValue result = TernaryValue.UNKNOWN;
671 |         if (n.getType() == Token.EQ || n.getType() == Token.NE) {
672 |           result = leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
673 |           if (n.isNE()) {
674 |             result = result.not();
675 |           }
676 |         } else {
677 |           // SHEQ or SHNE
678 |           if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
679 |                   rightTypeRestricted)) {
680 |             result = n.getType() == Token.SHEQ ?
681 |                 TernaryValue.FALSE : TernaryValue.TRUE;
682 |           }
683 |         }
684 | 
685 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
686 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
687 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
688 |         }
689 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
690 |         break;
691 |       }
692 | 
693 |       case Token.LT:
694 |       case Token.LE:
695 |       case Token.GT:
696 |       case Token.GE:
697 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
698 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
699 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
700 |           validator.expectNumber(
701 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
702 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
703 |           validator.expectNumber(
704 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
705 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
706 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
707 |           // OK.
708 |         } else {
709 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
710 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
711 |           // should match a string context.
712 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
713 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
714 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
715 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
716 |           message = "right side of comparison";
717 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
718 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
719 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
720 |         }
721 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
722 |         break;
723 | 
724 |       case Token.IN:
725 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
726 |         right = n.getLastChild();
727 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
728 |         validator.expectString(t, left, getJSType(left), "left side of 'in'");
729 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
730 |         if (rightType.isStruct()) {
731 |           report(t, right, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
732 |         }
733 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
734 |         break;
735 | 
736 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
737 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
738 |         right = n.getLastChild();
739 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
740 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
741 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "deterministic instanceof yields false");
742 |         validator.expectActualObject(
743 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
744 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
745 |         break;
746 | 
747 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
748 |         visitAssign(t, n);
749 |         typeable = false;
750 |         break;
751 | 
752 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
753 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
754 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
755 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
756 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
757 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
758 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
759 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
760 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
761 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
762 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
763 |         checkPropCreation(t, n.getFirstChild());
764 |         // fall through
765 | 
766 |       case Token.LSH:
767 |       case Token.RSH:
768 |       case Token.URSH:
769 |       case Token.DIV:
770 |       case Token.MOD:
771 |       case Token.BITOR:
772 |       case Token.BITXOR:
773 |       case Token.BITAND:
774 |       case Token.SUB:
775 |       case Token.ADD:
776 |       case Token.MUL:
777 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
778 |         break;
779 | 
780 |       case Token.DELPROP:
781 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
782 |         break;
783 | 
784 |       case Token.CASE:
785 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
786 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
787 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
788 |         typeable = false;
789 |         break;
790 | 
791 |       case Token.WITH: {
792 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
793 |         childType = getJSType(child);
794 |         validator.expectObject(t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
795 |         typeable = false;
796 |         break;
797 |       }
798 | 
799 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
800 |         visitFunction(t, n);
801 |         break;
802 | 
803 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
804 |       case Token.LABEL:
805 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
806 |       case Token.SWITCH:
807 |       case Token.BREAK:
808 |       case Token.CATCH:
809 |       case Token.TRY:
810 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
811 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
812 |       case Token.BLOCK:
813 |       case Token.EMPTY:
814 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
815 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
816 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
817 |       case Token.THROW:
818 |         typeable = false;
819 |         break;
820 | 
821 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
822 |       case Token.DO:
823 |       case Token.IF:
824 |       case Token.WHILE:
825 |         typeable = false;
826 |         break;
827 | 
828 |       case Token.FOR:
829 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
830 |           Node obj = n.getChildAtIndex(1);
831 |           if (getJSType(obj).isStruct()) {
832 |             report(t, obj, IN_USED_WITH_STRUCT);
833 |           }
834 |         }
835 |         typeable = false;
836 |         break;
837 | 
838 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
839 |       case Token.AND:
840 |       case Token.HOOK:
841 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
842 |       case Token.OR:
843 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
844 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
845 |         } else {
846 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
847 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
848 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
849 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
850 |           } else {
851 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
855 |           JSType typ = getJSType(n);
856 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
857 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n, typ);
858 |           }
859 |         }
860 |         break;
861 | 
862 |       default:
863 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
864 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
865 |         break;
866 |     }
867 | 
868 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
869 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
870 | 
871 |     if (typeable) {
872 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
873 |     }
874 | 
875 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
876 |   }

88 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,

90 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap) {
91 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());

104 |     // type is VOID.

===== M0032 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.finishConstructorDefinition(Node,String,FunctionType,Scope,CompilerInput,Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1262-1306
Documentation:

Method code:
1262 |     private void finishConstructorDefinition(
1263 |         Node n, String variableName, FunctionType fnType,
1264 |         Scope scopeToDeclareIn, CompilerInput input, Var newVar) {
1265 |       // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1266 |       FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1267 |       Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1268 | 
1269 |       // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1270 |       // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1271 |       // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1272 |       // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1273 |       // the same node.
1274 |       //
1275 |       // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1276 |       // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1277 |       prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1278 | 
1279 |       String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1280 | 
1281 |       // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1282 |       // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1283 |       // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1284 |       Var prototypeVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(prototypeName);
1285 |       if (prototypeVar != null && prototypeVar.scope == scopeToDeclareIn) {
1286 |         scopeToDeclareIn.undeclare(prototypeVar);
1287 |       }
1288 | 
1289 |       scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1290 |           n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1291 |           /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1292 |           superClassCtor == null ||
1293 |           superClassCtor.getInstanceType().isEquivalentTo(
1294 |               getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1295 | 
1296 |       // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1297 |       // it constructs itself.
1298 |       if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1299 |           !n.isFromExterns()) {
1300 |         compiler.report(
1301 |             JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1302 |                 fnType.isConstructor() ?
1303 |                 CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1304 |                 variableName));
1305 |       }
1306 |     }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParametersType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2061-2125
Documentation:
// order-checking in two places, we just do all of it in type resolution.
Method code:
2061 |   private Node parseParametersType(JsDocToken token) {
2062 |     Node paramsType = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
2063 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
2064 |     Node paramType = null;
2065 |     if (token != JsDocToken.RP) {
2066 |       do {
2067 |         if (paramType != null) {
2068 |           // skip past the comma
2069 |           next();
2070 |           skipEOLs();
2071 |           token = next();
2072 |         }
2073 | 
2074 |         if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
2075 |           // In the latest ES4 proposal, there are no type constraints allowed
2076 |           // on variable arguments. We support the old syntax for backwards
2077 |           // compatibility, but we should gradually tear it out.
2078 |           skipEOLs();
2079 |           if (match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2080 |             paramType = newNode(Token.ELLIPSIS);
2081 |           } else {
2082 |             skipEOLs();
2083 |             if (!match(JsDocToken.LB)) {
2084 |               return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.lb");
2085 |             }
2086 | 
2087 |             next();
2088 |             skipEOLs();
2089 |             paramType = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, parseTypeExpression(next()));
2090 |             skipEOLs();
2091 |             if (!match(JsDocToken.RB)) {
2092 |               return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
2093 |             }
2094 |             skipEOLs();
2095 |             next();
2096 |           }
2097 | 
2098 |           isVarArgs = true;
2099 |         } else {
2100 |           paramType = parseTypeExpression(token);
2101 |           if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
2102 |             skipEOLs();
2103 |             next();
2104 |             paramType = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, paramType);
2105 |           }
2106 |         }
2107 | 
2108 |         if (paramType == null) {
2109 |           return null;
2110 |         }
2111 |         paramsType.addChildToBack(paramType);
2112 |         if (isVarArgs) {
2113 |           break;
2114 |         }
2115 |       } while (match(JsDocToken.COMMA));
2116 |     }
2117 | 
2118 |     if (isVarArgs && match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2119 |       return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs");
2120 |     }
2121 | 
2122 |     // The right paren will be checked by parseFunctionType
2123 | 
2124 |     return paramsType;
2125 |   }

===== M0035 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 912-1021
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
912 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
913 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
914 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
915 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
916 | 
917 |     // Check property sets to 'object.property' when 'object' is known.
918 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
919 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
920 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
921 |       Node property = lvalue.getLastChild();
922 |       String pname = property.getString();
923 | 
924 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
925 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
926 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
927 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
928 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
929 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
930 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, pname, lvalue, rvalue);
931 |         }
932 |       }
933 | 
934 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
935 |       checkPropCreation(t, lvalue);
936 | 
937 |       // Prototype assignments are special, because they actually affect
938 |       // the definition of a class. These are mostly validated
939 |       // during TypedScopeCreator, and we only look for the "dumb" cases here.
940 |       // object.prototype = ...;
941 |       if (pname.equals("prototype")) {
942 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
943 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
944 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
945 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
946 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
947 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
948 |             // Only assign structs to the prototype of a @struct constructor
949 |             if (functionType.makesStructs() && !rvalueType.isStruct()) {
950 |               String funName = functionType.getTypeOfThis().toString();
951 |               compiler.report(t.makeError(assign, CONFLICTING_SHAPE_TYPE,
952 |                                           "struct", funName));
953 |             }
954 |             return;
955 |           }
956 |         }
957 |       }
958 | 
959 |       // The generic checks for 'object.property' when 'object' is known,
960 |       // and 'property' is declared on it.
961 |       // object.property = ...;
962 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
963 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
964 |       if (type != null) {
965 |         if (type.hasProperty(pname) &&
966 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(pname) &&
967 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, pname)) {
968 |           JSType expectedType = type.getPropertyType(pname);
969 |           if (!expectedType.isUnknownType()) {
970 |             validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
971 |                 t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
972 |                 expectedType, object, pname);
973 |             checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
974 |                 t, assign, object, pname, info, expectedType);
975 |             return;
976 |           }
977 |         }
978 |       }
979 | 
980 |       // If we couldn't get the property type with normal object property
981 |       // lookups, then check inheritance anyway with the unknown type.
982 |       checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
983 |           t, assign, object, pname, info, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
984 |     }
985 | 
986 |     // Check qualified name sets to 'object' and 'object.property'.
987 |     // This can sometimes handle cases when the type of 'object' is not known.
988 |     // e.g.,
989 |     // var obj = createUnknownType();
990 |     // /** @type {number} */ obj.foo = true;
991 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
992 |     if (lvalue.isQualifiedName()) {
993 |       // variable with inferred type case
994 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getQualifiedName());
995 |       if (var != null) {
996 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
997 |           return;
998 |         }
999 | 
1000 |         if (NodeUtil.getRootOfQualifiedName(lvalue).isThis() &&
1001 |             t.getScope() != var.getScope()) {
1002 |           // Don't look at "this.foo" variables from other scopes.
1003 |           return;
1004 |         }
1005 | 
1006 |         if (var.getType() != null) {
1007 |           leftType = var.getType();
1008 |         }
1009 |       }
1010 |     }
1011 | 
1012 |     // Fall through case for arbitrary LHS and arbitrary RHS.
1013 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
1014 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
1015 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1016 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
1017 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
1018 |     } else {
1019 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
1020 |     }
1021 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.LinkedFlowScope(FlatFlowScopeCache)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1413-1486
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1413 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1414 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1415 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1416 |       if (relationship != null) {
1417 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1418 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1419 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1420 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1421 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1422 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1423 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1424 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1425 | 
1426 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1427 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1428 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1429 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1430 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1431 | 
1432 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1433 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1434 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1435 |           }
1436 | 
1437 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1438 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1439 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1440 |           }
1441 |         }
1442 |       }
1443 | 
1444 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1445 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1446 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1447 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1448 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1449 |         if (objectType != null) {
1450 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1451 | 
1452 |           if (functionType != null) {
1453 |             FunctionType getterType =
1454 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1455 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1456 |                 objectType);
1457 |           }
1458 |         }
1459 |       }
1460 | 
1461 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1462 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1463 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1464 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1465 |       }
1466 | 
1467 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1468 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(n);
1469 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1470 |         if (objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType == null) {
1471 |           ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1472 |               typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1473 |           if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1474 |             setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1475 |             objectLiteralCast.objectNode.putBooleanProp(
1476 |                 Node.REFLECTED_OBJECT, true);
1477 |           } else {
1478 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1479 |                     CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1480 |           }
1481 |         } else {
1482 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1483 |                   objectLiteralCast.diagnosticType));
1484 |         }
1485 |       }
1486 |     }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1278 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1279 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1280 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1281 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1282 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1283 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1284 |       } else {
1285 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1286 |       }
1287 |     }
1288 |     return builder.build();
1289 |   }

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
216 |   boolean parse() {
217 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
218 |     skipEOLs();
219 | 
220 |     JsDocToken token = next();
221 | 
222 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
223 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
224 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
225 |       token = blockInfo.token;
226 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
227 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
228 |       }
229 |     } else {

231 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
232 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
233 |         // what it was.
234 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
235 |       }
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     return parseHelperLoop(token, Lists.<ExtendedTypeInfo>newArrayList());
239 |   }

===== M0067 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2515-2531
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2515 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2516 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2517 |     int c;
2518 |     while (true) {
2519 |       c = stream.getChar();
2520 |       if (c == ' ') {
2521 |         continue;
2522 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2523 |         matchedLc = true;
2524 |         break;
2525 |       } else {
2526 |         break;
2527 |       }
2528 |     }
2529 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2530 |     return matchedLc;
2531 |   }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 127-178
Documentation:

Method code:
127 |     @Override
128 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
129 |       // null is the smallest value
130 |       if (p2 == null) {
131 |         if (p1 == null) {
132 |           return 0;
133 |         } else {
134 |           return P1_GT_P2;
135 |         }
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // check level
139 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
140 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // sourceName comparison
144 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
145 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
146 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
147 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
148 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
149 |           return sourceCompare;
150 |         }
151 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
152 |         return P1_LT_P2;
153 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
154 |         return P1_GT_P2;
155 |       }
156 |       // lineno comparison
157 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
158 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
159 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
160 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
161 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
162 |         return P1_LT_P2;
163 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
164 |         return P1_GT_P2;
165 |       }
166 |       // charno comparison
167 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
168 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
169 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
170 |         return charno1 - charno2;
171 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
172 |         return P1_LT_P2;
173 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
174 |         return P1_GT_P2;
175 |       }
176 |       // description
177 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
178 |     }

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFunctionType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1967-2037
Documentation:
/**
   * FunctionType := 'function' FunctionSignatureType
   * FunctionSignatureType :=
   *    TypeParameters '(' 'this' ':' TypeName, ParametersType ')' ResultType
   */
Method code:
1967 |   private Node parseFunctionType(JsDocToken token) {
1968 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): We're not implementing generics at the moment, so
1969 |     // just throw out TypeParameters.
1970 |     if (token != JsDocToken.LP) {
1971 |       restoreLookAhead(token);
1972 |       return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.lp");
1973 |     }
1974 | 
1975 |     Node functionType = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
1976 |     Node parameters = null;
1977 |     skipEOLs();
1978 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
1979 |       token = next();
1980 | 
1981 |       boolean hasParams = true;
1982 |       if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1983 |         String tokenStr = stream.getString();
1984 |         boolean isThis = "this".equals(tokenStr);
1985 |         boolean isNew = "new".equals(tokenStr);
1986 |         if (isThis || isNew) {
1987 |           if (match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
1988 |             next();
1989 |             skipEOLs();
1990 |             Node contextType = wrapNode(
1991 |                 isThis ? Token.THIS : Token.NEW,
1992 |                 parseTypeName(next()));
1993 |             if (contextType == null) {
1994 |               return null;
1995 |             }
1996 | 
1997 |             functionType.addChildToFront(contextType);
1998 |           } else {
1999 |             return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.colon");
2000 |           }
2001 | 
2002 |           if (match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2003 |             next();
2004 |             skipEOLs();
2005 |             token = next();
2006 |           } else {
2007 |             hasParams = false;
2008 |           }
2009 |         }
2010 |       }
2011 | 
2012 |       if (hasParams) {
2013 |         parameters = parseParametersType(token);
2014 |         if (parameters == null) {
2015 |           return null;
2016 |         }
2017 |       }
2018 |     }
2019 | 
2020 |     if (parameters != null) {
2021 |       functionType.addChildToBack(parameters);
2022 |     }
2023 | 
2024 |     skipEOLs();
2025 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.RP)) {
2026 |       return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rp");
2027 |     }
2028 | 
2029 |     skipEOLs();
2030 |     Node resultType = parseResultType(next());
2031 |     if (resultType == null) {
2032 |       return null;
2033 |     } else {
2034 |       functionType.addChildToBack(resultType);
2035 |     }
2036 |     return functionType;
2037 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3118-3139
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3118 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3119 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3120 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3121 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3122 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3123 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3124 |       return parent;
3125 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3126 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3127 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3128 |       return parent;
3129 |     } else if (
3130 |         (parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3131 |         parent.isOr() ||
3132 |         parent.isAnd() ||
3133 |         (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3134 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3135 |     } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3136 |       return getBestLValue(parent);
3137 |     }
3138 |     return null;
3139 |   }

===== M0081 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 391-435
Documentation:

Method code:
391 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
392 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
393 |       cursor++;
394 |       --ungetCursor;
395 |       if (charno == -1) {
396 |         charno = getOffset();
397 |       }
398 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
399 |     }
400 | 
401 |     for (;;) {
402 |       int c;
403 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
404 |         hitEOF = true;
405 |         if (charno == -1) {
406 |           charno = getOffset();
407 |         }
408 |         return EOF_CHAR;
409 |       }
410 |       cursor++;
411 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
412 | 
413 | 
414 |       if (c <= 127) {
415 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
416 |           lineEndChar = c;
417 |           c = '\n';
418 |         }
419 |       } else {
420 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
421 |           continue;
422 |         }
423 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
424 |           lineEndChar = c;
425 |           c = '\n';
426 |         }
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       if (charno == -1) {
430 |         charno = getOffset();
431 |       }
432 | 
433 |       return c;
434 |     }
435 |   }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

423 |     @Override
424 |     public Bind describeFunctionBind(Node n, boolean useTypeInfo) {
425 |       if (!n.isCall()) {
426 |         return null;
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
430 |       String name = callTarget.getQualifiedName();
431 |       if (name != null) {
432 |         if (name.equals("Function.prototype.bind.call")) {
433 |           // goog.bind(fn, self, args...);
434 |           Node fn = callTarget.getNext();
435 |           if (fn == null) {
436 |             return null;
437 |           }
438 |           Node thisValue = safeNext(fn);
439 |           Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
440 |           return new Bind(fn, thisValue, parameters);
441 |         }
442 |       }
443 |

445 |           && callTarget.getLastChild().getString().equals("bind")) {
446 |         Node maybeFn = callTarget.getFirstChild();
447 |         JSType maybeFnType = maybeFn.getJSType();
448 |         FunctionType fnType = null;

450 |           fnType = maybeFnType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
451 |               .toMaybeFunctionType();

455 |           // (function(){}).bind(self, args...);
456 |           Node thisValue = callTarget.getNext();
457 |           Node parameters = safeNext(thisValue);
458 |           return new Bind(maybeFn, thisValue, parameters);
459 |         }
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       return null;
463 |     }

===== M0099 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 627-657
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
627 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
628 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
629 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
630 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
631 |     if (isOptional) {
632 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
633 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
634 |       // error can be reported.
635 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
636 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
637 |         emittedWarning = true;
638 |       }
639 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
640 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
641 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
642 |         emittedWarning = true;
643 |       }
644 |     } else {
645 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
646 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
647 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
648 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
649 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
650 |         } else {
651 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
652 |         }
653 |         emittedWarning = true;
654 |       }
655 |     }
656 |     return emittedWarning;
657 |   }

/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0111 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

===== M0119 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.filterNoResolvedType(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1016-1043
Documentation:
/**
   * When computing infima, we may get a situation like
   * inf(Type1, Type2)
   * where both types are unresolved, so they're technically
   * subtypes of one another.
   *
   * If this happens, filter them down to NoResolvedType.
   */
Method code:
1016 |   static JSType filterNoResolvedType(JSType type) {
1017 |     if (type.isNoResolvedType()) {
1018 |       // inf(UnresolvedType1, UnresolvedType2) needs to resolve
1019 |       // to the base unresolved type, so that the relation is symmetric.
1020 |       return type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE);
1021 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
1022 |       UnionType unionType = type.toMaybeUnionType();
1023 |       boolean needsFiltering = false;
1024 |       for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1025 |         if (alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1026 |           needsFiltering = true;
1027 |           break;
1028 |         }
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       if (needsFiltering) {
1032 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(type.registry);
1033 |         builder.addAlternate(type.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_RESOLVED_TYPE));
1034 |         for (JSType alt : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1035 |           if (!alt.isNoResolvedType()) {
1036 |             builder.addAlternate(alt);
1037 |           }
1038 |         }
1039 |         return builder.build();
1040 |       }
1041 |     }
1042 |     return type;
1043 |   }

===== M0127 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1320-1381
Documentation:

Method code:
1320 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1321 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1322 |     // We often have a couple of different types to choose from for the
1323 |     // property. Ordered by accuracy, we have
1324 |     // 1) A locally inferred qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1325 |     // 2) A globally declared qualified name (which is in the FlowScope)
1326 |     // 3) A property on the owner type (which is on objType)
1327 |     // 4) A name in the type registry (as a last resort)
1328 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1329 |     boolean isLocallyInferred = false;
1330 | 
1331 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1332 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1333 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1334 |     if (var != null) {
1335 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1336 |       if (varType != null) {
1337 |         boolean isDeclared = !var.isTypeInferred();
1338 |         isLocallyInferred = (var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName));
1339 |         if (isDeclared || isLocallyInferred) {
1340 |           propertyType = varType;
1341 |         }
1342 |       }
1343 |     }
1344 | 
1345 |     if (propertyType == null && objType != null) {
1346 |       JSType foundType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1347 |       if (foundType != null) {
1348 |         propertyType = foundType;
1349 |       }
1350 |     }
1351 | 
1352 |     if (propertyType != null && objType != null) {
1353 |       JSType restrictedObjType = objType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1354 |       if (!restrictedObjType.getTemplateTypeMap().isEmpty()
1355 |           && propertyType.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
1356 |         TemplateTypeMap typeMap = restrictedObjType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1357 |         TemplateTypeMapReplacer replacer = new TemplateTypeMapReplacer(
1358 |             registry, typeMap);
1359 |         propertyType = propertyType.visit(replacer);
1360 |       }
1361 |     }
1362 | 
1363 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType())
1364 |         && qualifiedName != null) {
1365 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1366 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1367 |       if (regType != null) {
1368 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1369 |       }
1370 |     }
1371 | 
1372 |     if (propertyType == null) {
1373 |       return unknownType;
1374 |     } else if (propertyType.isEquivalentTo(unknownType) && isLocallyInferred) {
1375 |       // If the type has been checked in this scope,
1376 |       // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1377 |       return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1378 |     } else {
1379 |       return propertyType;
1380 |     }
1381 |   }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 249-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
249 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
250 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
251 |     if (oldType == null) {
252 |       return this;
253 |     }
254 | 
255 |     // Propagate the template types, if they exist.
256 |     templateTypeNames = oldType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys();
257 | 
258 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
259 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
260 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
261 |       // Not a function literal.
262 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
263 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
264 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
265 |       }
266 |     } else {
267 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
268 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
269 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
270 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
271 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
272 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
273 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
274 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
275 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
276 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
277 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
278 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
279 | 
280 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
281 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
282 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
283 | 
284 |           // The subclass method might write its var_args as individual
285 |           // arguments.
286 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
287 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
288 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
289 |           }
290 |         } else {
291 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
292 |               paramBuilder,
293 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
294 |               warnedAboutArgList,
295 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
296 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
297 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
298 |         }
299 |       }
300 | 
301 |       // Clone any remaining params that aren't in the function literal,
302 |       // but make them optional.
303 |       while (oldParams.hasNext()) {
304 |         paramBuilder.newOptionalParameterFromNode(oldParams.next());
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
308 |     }
309 |     return this;
310 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

===== M0138 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseNew(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1212-1248
Documentation:

Method code:
1212 |   private FlowScope traverseNew(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1213 |     scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
1214 | 
1215 |     Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
1216 |     JSType constructorType = constructor.getJSType();
1217 |     JSType type = null;
1218 |     if (constructorType != null) {
1219 |       constructorType = constructorType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1220 |       if (constructorType.isUnknownType()) {
1221 |         type = unknownType;
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         FunctionType ct = constructorType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1224 |         if (ct == null && constructorType instanceof FunctionType) {
1225 |           // If constructorType is a NoObjectType, then toMaybeFunctionType will
1226 |           // return null. But NoObjectType implements the FunctionType
1227 |           // interface, precisely because it can validly construct objects.
1228 |           ct = (FunctionType) constructorType;
1229 |         }
1230 |         if (ct != null && ct.isConstructor()) {
1231 |           backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(n, ct);
1232 | 
1233 |           // If necessary, create a TemplatizedType wrapper around the instance
1234 |           // type, based on the types of the constructor parameters.
1235 |           ObjectType instanceType = ct.getInstanceType();
1236 |           Map<TemplateType, JSType> inferredTypes =
1237 |               inferTemplateTypesFromParameters(ct, n);
1238 |           if (inferredTypes.isEmpty()) {
1239 |             type = instanceType;
1240 |           } else {
1241 |             type = registry.createTemplatizedType(instanceType, inferredTypes);
1242 |           }
1243 |         }
1244 |       }
1245 |     }
1246 |     n.setJSType(type);
1247 |     return scope;
1248 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not

===== M0155 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 94-155
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
94 |   @Override
95 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
96 |     SubclassRelationship relationship =
97 |         super.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
98 |     if (relationship != null) {
99 |       return relationship;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
103 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
104 |     if (type != null) {
105 |       Node subclass = null;
106 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
107 | 
108 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
109 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
110 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
111 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
112 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
113 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
114 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
115 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
116 |           callName.isGetProp();
117 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
118 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
119 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
120 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
121 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
122 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
123 |       } else {
124 |         return null;
125 |       }
126 | 
127 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
128 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
129 |         // inheritance.
130 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
131 |           return null;
132 |         }
133 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
134 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
135 |             return null;
136 |           }
137 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
138 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
139 |         }
140 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
141 |       }
142 | 
143 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
144 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
145 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
146 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
147 |       if (subclass != null &&
148 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
149 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
150 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
151 |       }
152 |     }
153 | 
154 |     return null;
155 |   }

===== M0166 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 112-166
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
112 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
113 |     switch (n.getType()) {
114 |       case Token.VAR:
115 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
116 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |              child != null;) {
118 |           Node next = child.getNext();
119 |           declareVar(child);
120 |           child = next;
121 |         }
122 |         return;
123 | 
124 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
125 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
126 |           return;
127 |         }
128 | 
129 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
130 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
131 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
135 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
136 | 
137 |       case Token.CATCH:
138 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
139 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
140 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
141 |         // is the code block
142 | 
143 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
144 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
145 | 
146 |         declareVar(var);
147 |         scanVars(block);
148 |         return;  // only one child to scan
149 | 
150 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
151 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
152 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
153 |         break;
154 |     }
155 | 
156 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
157 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
158 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
159 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
160 |            child != null;) {
161 |         Node next = child.getNext();
162 |         scanVars(child);
163 |         child = next;
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1000-1029
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
1000 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
1001 |     int i = 0;
1002 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
1003 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
1004 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
1005 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
1006 |         return;
1007 |       }
1008 | 
1009 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
1010 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
1011 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
1012 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
1013 | 
1014 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Filter out non-function types
1015 |       // (such as null and undefined) as
1016 |       // we only care about FUNCTION subtypes here.
1017 |       JSType restrictedParameter = iParameterType
1018 |           .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
1019 |           .toMaybeFunctionType();
1020 |       if (restrictedParameter != null) {
1021 |         if (iArgument.isFunction() &&
1022 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
1023 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
1024 |           iArgument.setJSType(restrictedParameter);
1025 |         }
1026 |       }
1027 |       i++;
1028 |     }
1029 |   }

===== M0180 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 662-724
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
662 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
663 |     if (returnType == null) {
664 |       // Infer return types.
665 |       // We need to be extremely conservative about this, because of two
666 |       // competing needs.
667 |       // 1) If we infer the return type of f too widely, then we won't be able
668 |       //    to assign f to other functions.
669 |       // 2) If we infer the return type of f too narrowly, then we won't be
670 |       //    able to override f in subclasses.
671 |       // So we only infer in cases where the user doesn't expect to write
672 |       // @return annotations--when it's very obvious that the function returns
673 |       // nothing.
674 |       if (!contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() &&
675 |           !contents.mayHaveSingleThrow() &&
676 |           !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
677 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
678 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
679 |       }
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     if (returnType == null) {
683 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
684 |     }
685 | 
686 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
687 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
688 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
689 |     }
690 | 
691 |     FunctionType fnType;
692 |     if (isConstructor) {
693 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
694 |     } else if (isInterface) {
695 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
696 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), classTemplateTypeNames);
697 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
698 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
699 |       }
700 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
701 |     } else {
702 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
703 |           .withName(fnName)
704 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
705 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
706 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
707 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
708 |           .withTemplateKeys(templateTypeNames)
709 |           .build();
710 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
711 |     }
712 | 
713 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
714 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
715 |     }
716 | 
717 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
718 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
719 |     }
720 | 
721 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
722 | 
723 |     return fnType;
724 |   }

/**
     * Handle typedefs.
     * @param t The current traversal.

Method code:
1928 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1929 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1930 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1931 |         return;
1932 |       }
1933 |

1935 |       if (typedef == null) {
1936 |         return;

1941 |       // to handle these properly.

1949 |       }

===== M0184 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }
