===== M0194 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 57-193
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
57 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
58 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
59 |     int c;
60 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
61 |     for (;;) {
62 |       // eat white spaces
63 |       for (;;) {
64 |         charno = -1;
65 |         c = getChar();
66 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
67 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
68 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
70 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
71 |           break;
72 |         }
73 |       }
74 | 
75 |       switch (c) {
76 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
77 |         case '@':
78 |           do {
79 |             c = getChar();
80 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
81 |               addToString(c);
82 |             } else {
83 |               ungetChar(c);
84 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
85 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
86 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
87 |             }
88 |           } while (true);
89 | 
90 |         case '*':
91 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
92 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
93 |           } else {
94 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
95 |           }
96 | 
97 |         case ',':
98 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
99 | 
100 |         case '>':
101 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
102 | 
103 |         case '(':
104 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
105 | 
106 |         case ')':
107 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
108 | 
109 |         case '{':
110 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
111 | 
112 |         case '}':
113 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
114 | 
115 |         case '[':
116 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
117 | 
118 |         case ']':
119 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
120 | 
121 |         case '?':
122 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
123 | 
124 |         case '!':
125 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
126 | 
127 |         case ':':
128 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
129 | 
130 |         case '=':
131 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
132 | 
133 |         case '|':
134 |           matchChar('|');
135 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
136 | 
137 |         case '.':
138 |           c = getChar();
139 |           if (c == '<') {
140 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
141 |           } else {
142 |             if (c == '.') {
143 |               c = getChar();
144 |               if (c == '.') {
145 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
146 |               } else {
147 |                 addToString('.');
148 |               }
149 |             }
150 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
151 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
152 |             c = '.';
153 |           }
154 |           // fall through
155 | 
156 |         default: {
157 |           // recognize a JsDoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
158 |           // a non-JsDoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
159 |           int c1 = c;
160 |           addToString(c);
161 |           int c2 = getChar();
162 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
163 |             ungetChar(c2);
164 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
165 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
166 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
167 |           } else {
168 |             do {
169 |               c1 = c2;
170 |               c2 = getChar();
171 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
172 |                 ungetChar(c2);
173 |                 ungetChar(c1);
174 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
175 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
176 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
177 |               } else {
178 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
179 |                   addToString(c1);
180 |                 } else {
181 |                   ungetChar(c2);
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
184 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
185 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
186 |                 }
187 |               }
188 |             } while (true);
189 |           }
190 |         }
191 |       }
192 |     }
193 |   }

===== M0195 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 2054-2073
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
2054 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
2055 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
2056 |     Preconditions.checkState(!n.isFunction() ||
2057 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
2058 |             type.isUnknownType());
2059 |     // TODO(johnlenz): this seems like a strange place to check "@implicitCast"
2060 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
2061 |     if (info != null) {
2062 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
2063 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
2064 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
2065 |         compiler.report(
2066 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
2067 |       }
2068 |     }
2069 | 
2070 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
2071 |       n.setJSType(type);
2072 |     }
2073 |   }

Method code:
443 |     @Override
444 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
445 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
446 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
447 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
448 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
449 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
450 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
451 |           return true;
452 |         }
453 |       }
454 |       return false;
455 |     }

===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 200-259
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
200 |   @Override
201 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
202 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
203 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
204 |     // show up in the type registry.
205 |     Scope newScope = null;
206 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
207 |     if (parent == null) {
208 |       JSType globalThis =
209 |           typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
210 | 
211 |       // Mark the main root, the externs root, and the src root
212 |       // with the global this type.
213 |       root.setJSType(globalThis);
214 |       root.getFirstChild().setJSType(globalThis);
215 |       root.getLastChild().setJSType(globalThis);
216 | 
217 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
218 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
219 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
220 | 
221 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
222 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
223 | 
224 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
225 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
226 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
227 |     } else {
228 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
229 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
230 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
231 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
235 | 
236 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
237 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
238 |     // build properties on.
239 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
240 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
241 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
242 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
243 |         JSType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
244 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
245 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
246 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
247 |         }
248 |       }
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     if (parent == null) {
252 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
253 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
254 |           delegateCallingConventions);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     newScope.setTypeResolver(scopeBuilder);
258 |     return newScope;
259 |   }

/**
     * Declares all of a function's arguments.
     */
Method code:
2042 |     private void declareArguments(Node functionNode) {
2043 |       Node astParameters = functionNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
2044 |       Node iifeArgumentNode = null;
2045 | 
2046 |       if (NodeUtil.isCallOrNewTarget(functionNode)) {
2047 |         iifeArgumentNode = functionNode.getNext();
2048 |       }
2049 | 
2050 |       FunctionType functionType =
2051 |           JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(functionNode.getJSType());
2052 |       if (functionType != null) {
2053 |         Node jsDocParameters = functionType.getParametersNode();
2054 |         if (jsDocParameters != null) {
2055 |           Node jsDocParameter = jsDocParameters.getFirstChild();
2056 |           for (Node astParameter : astParameters.children()) {
2057 |             JSType paramType = jsDocParameter == null ?
2058 |                 unknownType : jsDocParameter.getJSType();
2059 |             boolean inferred = paramType == null || paramType == unknownType;
2060 | 
2061 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null && inferred) {
2062 |               String argumentName = iifeArgumentNode.getQualifiedName();
2063 |               Var argumentVar =
2064 |                   argumentName == null || scope.getParent() == null
2065 |                   ? null : scope.getParent().getVar(argumentName);
2066 |               if (argumentVar != null && !argumentVar.isTypeInferred()) {
2067 |                 paramType = argumentVar.getType();
2068 |               }
2069 |             }
2070 | 
2071 |             if (paramType == null) {
2072 |               paramType = unknownType;
2073 |             }
2074 | 
2075 |             defineSlot(astParameter, functionNode, paramType, inferred);
2076 | 
2077 |             if (jsDocParameter != null) {
2078 |               jsDocParameter = jsDocParameter.getNext();
2079 |             }
2080 |             if (iifeArgumentNode != null) {
2081 |               iifeArgumentNode = iifeArgumentNode.getNext();
2082 |             }
2083 |           }
2084 |         }
2085 |       }

===== M0229 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.maybeResolveTemplatedType(JSType,JSType,Map<TemplateType, JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1057-1113
Documentation:

Method code:
1057 |   private void maybeResolveTemplatedType(
1058 |       JSType paramType,
1059 |       JSType argType,
1060 |       Map<TemplateType, JSType> resolvedTypes) {
1061 |     if (paramType.isTemplateType()) {
1062 |       // @param {T}
1063 |       resolvedTemplateType(
1064 |           resolvedTypes, paramType.toMaybeTemplateType(), argType);
1065 |     } else if (paramType.isUnionType()) {
1066 |       // @param {Array.<T>|NodeList|Arguments|{length:number}}
1067 |       UnionType unionType = paramType.toMaybeUnionType();
1068 |       for (JSType alernative : unionType.getAlternates()) {
1069 |         maybeResolveTemplatedType(alernative, argType, resolvedTypes);
1070 |       }
1071 |     } else if (paramType.isFunctionType()) {
1072 |       FunctionType paramFunctionType = paramType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1073 |       FunctionType argFunctionType = argType
1074 |           .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
1075 |           .collapseUnion()
1076 |           .toMaybeFunctionType();
1077 |       if (argFunctionType != null && argFunctionType.isSubtype(paramType)) {
1078 |         // infer from return type of the function type
1079 |         maybeResolveTemplatedType(
1080 |             paramFunctionType.getTypeOfThis(),
1081 |             argFunctionType.getTypeOfThis(), resolvedTypes);
1082 |         // infer from return type of the function type
1083 |         maybeResolveTemplatedType(
1084 |             paramFunctionType.getReturnType(),
1085 |             argFunctionType.getReturnType(), resolvedTypes);
1086 |         // infer from parameter types of the function type
1087 |         maybeResolveTemplateTypeFromNodes(
1088 |             paramFunctionType.getParameters(),
1089 |             argFunctionType.getParameters(), resolvedTypes);
1090 |       }
1091 |     } else if (paramType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1092 |       // @param {Array.<T>}
1093 |       ObjectType referencedParamType = paramType
1094 |           .toMaybeTemplatizedType()
1095 |           .getReferencedType();
1096 |       JSType argObjectType = argType
1097 |           .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
1098 |           .collapseUnion();
1099 | 
1100 |       if (argObjectType.isSubtype(referencedParamType)) {
1101 |         // If the argument type is a subtype of the parameter type, resolve any
1102 |         // template types amongst their templatized types.
1103 |         TemplateTypeMap paramTypeMap = paramType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1104 |         TemplateTypeMap argTypeMap = argObjectType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1105 |         for (TemplateType key : paramTypeMap.getTemplateKeys()) {
1106 |           maybeResolveTemplatedType(
1107 |               paramTypeMap.getTemplateType(key),
1108 |               argTypeMap.getTemplateType(key),
1109 |               resolvedTypes);
1110 |         }
1111 |       }
1112 |     }
1113 |   }

===== M0231 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.getReadableJSTypeName(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 724-777
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a node, get a human-readable name for the type of that node so
   * that will be easy for the programmer to find the original declaration.
   *
   * For example, if SubFoo's property "bar" might have the human-readable
   * name "Foo.prototype.bar".
   *
   * @param n The node.
   * @param dereference If true, the type of the node will be dereferenced
   *     to an Object type, if possible.
   */
Method code:
724 |   String getReadableJSTypeName(Node n, boolean dereference) {
725 |     JSType type = getJSType(n);
726 |     if (dereference) {
727 |       ObjectType dereferenced = type.dereference();
728 |       if (dereferenced != null) {
729 |         type = dereferenced;
730 |       }
731 |     }
732 | 
733 |     // The best type name is the actual type name.
734 |     if (type.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
735 |         (type.toObjectType() != null &&
736 |          type.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null)) {
737 |       return type.toString();
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     // If we're analyzing a GETPROP, the property may be inherited by the
741 |     // prototype chain. So climb the prototype chain and find out where
742 |     // the property was originally defined.
743 |     if (n.isGetProp()) {
744 |       ObjectType objectType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild()).dereference();
745 |       if (objectType != null) {
746 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
747 |         if (objectType.getConstructor() != null &&
748 |             objectType.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
749 |           objectType = FunctionType.getTopDefiningInterface(
750 |               objectType, propName);
751 |         } else {
752 |           // classes
753 |           while (objectType != null && !objectType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
754 |             objectType = objectType.getImplicitPrototype();
755 |           }
756 |         }
757 | 
758 |         // Don't show complex function names or anonymous types.
759 |         // Instead, try to get a human-readable type name.
760 |         if (objectType != null &&
761 |             (objectType.getConstructor() != null ||
762 |              objectType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
763 |           return objectType.toString() + "." + propName;
764 |         }
765 |       }
766 |     }
767 | 
768 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
769 |     if (qualifiedName != null) {
770 |       return qualifiedName;
771 |     } else if (type.isFunctionType()) {
772 |       // Don't show complex function names.
773 |       return "function";
774 |     } else {
775 |       return type.toString();
776 |     }
777 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken,List<ExtendedTypeInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 241-286
Documentation:

Method code:
241 |   private boolean parseHelperLoop(JsDocToken token,
242 |                                   List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes) {
243 |     while (true) {
244 |       switch (token) {
245 |         case ANNOTATION:
246 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
247 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
248 |             token = parseAnnotation(token, extendedTypes);
249 |           } else {
250 |             token = next();
251 |           }
252 |           break;
253 | 
254 |         case EOC:
255 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
256 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
257 |           }
258 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
259 |           return true;
260 | 
261 |         case EOF:
262 |           // discard any accumulated information
263 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
264 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
265 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
266 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
267 |           return false;
268 | 
269 |         case EOL:
270 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
271 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
272 |           }
273 |           token = next();
274 |           break;
275 | 
276 |         default:
277 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
278 |             token = next();
279 |           } else {
280 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
281 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
282 |           }
283 |           break;
284 |       }
285 |     }
286 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node,String,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1597-1768
Documentation:
/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1597 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1598 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1599 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1600 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1601 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1602 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1603 | 
1604 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1605 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1606 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1607 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1608 | 
1609 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1610 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1611 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1612 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1613 |         }
1614 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1615 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1616 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1617 | 
1618 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1619 |         return createOptionalType(
1620 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1621 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1622 | 
1623 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1624 |         return createOptionalType(
1625 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1626 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1627 | 
1628 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1629 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1630 | 
1631 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1632 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1633 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1634 | 
1635 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1636 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1637 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1638 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1639 |           builder.addAlternate(
1640 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1641 |         }
1642 |         return builder.build();
1643 | 
1644 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1645 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1646 | 
1647 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1648 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1649 | 
1650 |       case Token.STRING:
1651 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1652 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1653 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1654 |             !(namedType instanceof NamespaceType) &&
1655 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1656 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1657 |           int nAllowedTypes =
1658 |               namedType.getTemplateTypeMap().numUnfilledTemplateKeys();
1659 |           if (typeList != null && nAllowedTypes > 0) {
1660 |             // Templatized types.
1661 |             ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> templateTypes =
1662 |                 ImmutableList.builder();
1663 | 
1664 |             // Special case for Object, where Object.<X> implies Object.<?,X>.
1665 |             if (n.getString().equals("Object") &&
1666 |                 typeList.getFirstChild() == typeList.getLastChild()) {
1667 |               templateTypes.add(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1668 |             }
1669 | 
1670 |             int templateNodeIndex = 0;
1671 |             for (Node templateNode : typeList.getFirstChild().siblings()) {
1672 |               // Don't parse more templatized type nodes than the type can
1673 |               // accommodate. This is because some existing clients have
1674 |               // template annotations on non-templatized classes, for instance:
1675 |               //   goog.structs.Set.<SomeType>
1676 |               // The problem in these cases is that the previously-unparsed
1677 |               // SomeType is not actually a valid type. To prevent these clients
1678 |               // from seeing unknown type errors, we explicitly don't parse
1679 |               // these types.
1680 |               // TODO(user): Address this issue by removing bad template
1681 |               // annotations on non-templatized classes.
1682 |               if (++templateNodeIndex > nAllowedTypes) {
1683 |                 break;
1684 |               }
1685 |               templateTypes.add(createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1686 |                   templateNode, sourceName, scope));
1687 |             }
1688 |             namedType = createTemplatizedType(
1689 |                 (ObjectType) namedType, templateTypes.build());
1690 |             Preconditions.checkNotNull(namedType);
1691 |           }
1692 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1693 |         } else {
1694 |           return namedType;
1695 |         }
1696 | 
1697 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1698 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1699 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1700 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1701 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1702 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1703 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1704 |           thisType =
1705 |               ObjectType.cast(
1706 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1707 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1708 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1709 |           if (thisType == null) {
1710 |             reporter.warning(
1711 |                 SimpleErrorReporter.getMessage0(
1712 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1713 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1714 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1715 |                 sourceName,
1716 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1717 |           }
1718 | 
1719 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1720 |           current = current.getNext();
1721 |         }
1722 | 
1723 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1724 | 
1725 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1726 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1727 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1728 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1729 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1730 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1731 |               } else {
1732 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1733 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1734 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1735 |               }
1736 |             } else {
1737 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1738 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1739 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1740 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1741 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1742 |                   reporter.warning(
1743 |                       SimpleErrorReporter.getMessage0(
1744 |                           "msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1745 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1746 |                 }
1747 |               } else {
1748 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1749 |               }
1750 |             }
1751 |           }
1752 |           current = current.getNext();
1753 |         }
1754 | 
1755 |         JSType returnType =
1756 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1757 | 
1758 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1759 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1760 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1761 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1762 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1763 |             .build();
1764 |     }
1765 | 
1766 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1767 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1768 |   }

===== M0246 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 873-1002
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the JsDoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
873 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
874 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
875 |         @Nullable String name,
876 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
877 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
878 | 
879 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
880 | 
881 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
882 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
883 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
884 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
885 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
886 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
887 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
888 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
889 |             functionType = aliasedType;
890 | 
891 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
892 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
893 |             }
894 |           }
895 |         }
896 |       }
897 | 
898 |       if (functionType == null) {
899 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
900 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
901 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
902 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
903 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
904 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
905 | 
906 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
907 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
908 | 
909 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
910 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
911 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
912 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
913 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         if (functionType == null) {
918 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
919 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
920 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
921 |           Var ownerVar = null;
922 |           String propName = null;
923 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
924 |           if (ownerName != null) {
925 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
926 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
927 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
928 |             }
929 |             if (name != null) {
930 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
931 |             }
932 |           }
933 | 
934 |           ObjectType prototypeOwner = getPrototypeOwnerType(ownerType);
935 |           TemplateTypeMap prototypeOwnerTypeMap = null;
936 |           if (prototypeOwner != null &&
937 |               prototypeOwner.getTypeOfThis() != null) {
938 |             prototypeOwnerTypeMap =
939 |                 prototypeOwner.getTypeOfThis().getTemplateTypeMap();
940 |           }
941 | 
942 |           FunctionType overriddenType = null;
943 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
944 |             overriddenType = findOverriddenFunction(
945 |                 ownerType, propName, prototypeOwnerTypeMap);
946 |           }
947 | 
948 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
949 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
950 |                   scope)
951 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
952 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenType, parametersNode)
953 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info, prototypeOwner)
954 |               .inferInheritance(info);
955 | 
956 |           if (info == null || !info.hasReturnType()) {
957 |             /**
958 |              * when there is no {@code @return} annotation, look for inline
959 |              * return type declaration
960 |              */
961 |             if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
962 |                 rValue.getFirstChild() != null) {
963 |               JSDocInfo nameDocInfo = rValue.getFirstChild().getJSDocInfo();
964 |               builder.inferReturnType(nameDocInfo, true);
965 |             }
966 |           } else {
967 |             builder.inferReturnType(info, false);
968 |           }
969 | 
970 |           // Infer the context type.
971 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
972 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType() &&
973 |               ownerType.getOwnerFunction().hasInstanceType()) {
974 |             builder.inferThisType(
975 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
976 |             searchedForThisType = true;
977 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
978 |             // If 'this' has a type, use that instead.
979 |             // This is a hack, necessary because CollectProperties (below)
980 |             // doesn't run with the scope that it's building,
981 |             // so scope.getTypeOfThis() will be wrong.
982 |             JSType injectedThisType = ownerNode.getJSType();
983 |             builder.inferThisType(
984 |                 info,
985 |                 injectedThisType == null ?
986 |                 scope.getTypeOfThis() : injectedThisType);
987 |             searchedForThisType = true;
988 |           }
989 | 
990 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
991 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
992 |           }
993 | 
994 |           functionType = builder
995 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
996 |               .buildAndRegister();
997 |         }
998 |       }
999 | 
1000 |       // all done
1001 |       return functionType;
1002 |     }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 201-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
201 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
202 |     initialize();
203 |     int step = 0;
204 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
205 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
206 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
207 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
208 |       }
209 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
210 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
211 |       joinInputs(curNode);
212 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
213 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
214 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
215 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
216 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
217 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
218 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
219 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
220 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
221 |           }
222 |         }
223 |       }
224 |       step++;
225 |     }
226 |     if (isForward()) {
227 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
228 |     }
229 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */

124 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());

126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);

129 |     }
130 |   }

   * @param properties A map of all the properties of this record type.
   * @param declared Whether this is a declared or synthesized type.
   *     A synthesized record type is just used for bookkeeping
   *     in the type system. A declared record type was actually used in the
   *     user's program.
   * @throws IllegalStateException if the {@code RecordProperty} associated
   *         with a property is null.

93 |             "RecordProperty associated with a property should not be null!");
94 |       }

96 |         defineDeclaredProperty(
97 |             property, prop.getType(), prop.getPropertyNode());
98 |       } else {
99 |         defineSynthesizedProperty(
100 |             property, prop.getType(), prop.getPropertyNode());

102 |     }

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1232-1234
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1232 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1233 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1234 |   }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1163-1260
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1163 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1164 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1165 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1166 | 
1167 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1168 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1169 |           isGlobalVar &&
1170 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1171 |            parent.isFunction());
1172 | 
1173 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1174 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1175 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1176 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1177 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1178 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1179 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1180 | 
1181 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1182 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1183 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1184 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1185 |         }
1186 |       }
1187 | 
1188 |       // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet. So read
1189 |       // the extern info from the node.
1190 |       Var newVar = null;
1191 | 
1192 |       // declared in closest scope?
1193 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1194 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1195 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1196 |         newVar = validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1197 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1198 |       } else {
1199 |         if (type != null) {
1200 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1201 |         }
1202 | 
1203 |         newVar =
1204 |           scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1205 | 
1206 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1207 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1208 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1209 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1210 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1211 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1212 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1213 |           }
1214 |         }
1215 |       }
1216 | 
1217 |       // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1218 |       FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1219 |       if (fnType != null &&
1220 |           // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1221 |           !type.isEmptyType()) {
1222 | 
1223 |         // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance type
1224 |         // (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1225 |         // This doesn't apply to structural constructors (like
1226 |         // function(new:Array). Checking the constructed type against
1227 |         // the variable name is a sufficient check for this.
1228 |         if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1229 |             variableName.equals(fnType.getReferenceName())) {
1230 |           finishConstructorDefinition(n, variableName, fnType, scopeToDeclareIn,
1231 |                                       input, newVar);
1232 |         }
1233 |       }
1234 | 
1235 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1236 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1237 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1238 |         if (inferred) {
1239 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1240 |               type == null ?
1241 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1242 |               type,
1243 |               n);
1244 |         } else {
1245 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1246 |         }
1247 |       }
1248 | 
1249 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1250 |           && type != null
1251 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1252 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1253 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1254 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1255 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1256 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1257 |         globalThisCtor
1258 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1259 |       }
1260 |     }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 569-637
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
569 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
570 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
571 |     Node obj = getprop.getFirstChild();
572 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(obj);
573 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
574 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
575 |     boolean propCreationInConstructor = obj.isThis() &&
576 |         getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor();
577 | 
578 |     if (objectType == null) {
579 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
580 |     } else {
581 |       if (nodeType.isStruct() && !objectType.hasProperty(propName)) {
582 |         // In general, we don't want to define a property on a struct object,
583 |         // b/c TypeCheck will later check for improper property creation on
584 |         // structs. There are two exceptions.
585 |         // 1) If it's a property created inside the constructor, on the newly
586 |         //    created instance, allow it.
587 |         // 2) If it's a prototype property, allow it. For example:
588 |         //    Foo.prototype.bar = baz;
589 |         //    where Foo.prototype is a struct and the assignment happens at the
590 |         //    top level and the constructor Foo is defined in the same file.
591 |         boolean staticPropCreation = false;
592 |         Node maybeAssignStm = getprop.getParent().getParent();
593 |         if (syntacticScope.isGlobal() &&
594 |             NodeUtil.isPrototypePropertyDeclaration(maybeAssignStm)) {
595 |           String propCreationFilename = maybeAssignStm.getSourceFileName();
596 |           Node ctor = objectType.getOwnerFunction().getSource();
597 |           if (ctor != null &&
598 |               ctor.getSourceFileName().equals(propCreationFilename)) {
599 |             staticPropCreation = true;
600 |           }
601 |         }
602 |         if (!propCreationInConstructor && !staticPropCreation) {
603 |           return; // Early return to avoid creating the property below.
604 |         }
605 |       }
606 | 
607 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
608 |         return;
609 |       }
610 | 
611 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
612 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
613 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
614 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the property.
615 |         //
616 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
617 |         //    inferred type.
618 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
619 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
620 |         //    define it.
621 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
622 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
623 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
624 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) || !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
625 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
626 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
627 |           } else {
628 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
629 |           }
630 |         } else if (propCreationInConstructor) {
631 |           objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, getprop);
632 |         } else {
633 |           registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
634 |         }
635 |       }
636 |     }
637 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1728-1763
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1728 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1729 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1730 | 
1731 |     skipEOLs();
1732 | 
1733 |     boolean restArg = false;
1734 |     token = next();
1735 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1736 |       token = next();
1737 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1738 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1739 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, IR.empty());
1740 |       }
1741 |       restArg = true;
1742 |     }
1743 | 
1744 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1745 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1746 |       skipEOLs();
1747 |       if (restArg) {
1748 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1749 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1750 |         next();
1751 |         skipEOLs();
1752 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1753 |       }
1754 | 
1755 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1756 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1757 |       } else {
1758 |         next();
1759 |       }
1760 |     }
1761 | 
1762 |     return typeNode;
1763 |   }

===== M0334 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 529-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isStruct()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 272-291
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true iff {@code this} can be a {@code struct}.
   * UnionType overrides the method, assume {@code this} is not a union here.
   */
Method code:
272 |   public boolean isStruct() {
273 |     if (isObject()) {
274 |       ObjectType objType = toObjectType();
275 |       ObjectType iproto = objType.getImplicitPrototype();
276 |       // For the case when a @struct constructor is assigned to a function's
277 |       // prototype property
278 |       if (iproto != null && iproto.isStruct()) {
279 |         return true;
280 |       }
281 |       FunctionType ctor = objType.getConstructor();
282 |       // This test is true for object literals
283 |       if (ctor == null) {
284 |         JSDocInfo info = objType.getJSDocInfo();
285 |         return info != null && info.makesStructs();
286 |       } else {
287 |         return ctor.makesStructs();
288 |       }
289 |     }
290 |     return false;
291 |   }

86 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {

89 |       if (composeGuard.demoteErrors) {

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 458-483
Documentation:

Method code:
458 |   @Override
459 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
460 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
461 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
462 |     switch (n.getType()) {
463 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
464 |         // normal type checking
465 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
466 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
467 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
468 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
469 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
470 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
471 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
472 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
473 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
474 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
475 |         }
476 | 
477 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
478 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
479 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
480 |         break;
481 |     }
482 |     return true;
483 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2075-2090
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as syntactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2075 |   public static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2076 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2077 |         n.isGetElem());
2078 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2079 |     if (parent == null) {
2080 |       return false;
2081 |     }
2082 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2083 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2084 |         || parent.isVar()
2085 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2086 |         || parent.isDec()
2087 |         || parent.isInc()
2088 |         || parent.isParamList()

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 830-857
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
830 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
831 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
832 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
833 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
834 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
835 |           return;
836 |         }
837 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
838 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
839 | 
840 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
841 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
842 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
843 |           } else {
844 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
845 |           }
846 |         } else { // Has a catch.
847 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
848 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
849 |             return;
850 |           } else {
851 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
852 |           }
853 |         }
854 |         lastJump = handler;
855 |       }
856 |     }
857 |   }

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 258-308
Documentation:

Method code:
258 |   @Override
259 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
260 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
261 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
262 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
263 |         return true;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
267 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
268 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate through all
269 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
270 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
271 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
272 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
273 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
274 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
275 |         return false;
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
279 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
280 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
281 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
282 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
283 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
284 |             return false;
285 |           }
286 |         }
287 | 
288 |         return true;
289 |       }
290 | 
291 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
292 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
293 | 
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
299 |       }
300 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
301 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
302 |           return false;
303 |         }
304 |       }
305 |       return true;
306 |     }
307 |     return false;
308 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1365-1394
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1365 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1366 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1367 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1368 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1369 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1370 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1371 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1372 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1373 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1374 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1375 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1376 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1377 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1378 |       return false;
1379 |     }
1380 | 
1381 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1382 |     if (type == null) {
1383 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1384 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1385 |       if (var != null) {
1386 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1387 |         if (varType != null) {
1388 |           type = varType;
1389 |         }
1390 |       }
1391 |     }
1392 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1393 |     return true;
1394 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPropertyType(String)

Method code:
601 |   @Override
602 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String name) {
603 |     if (!hasOwnProperty(name)) {
604 |       // Define the "call", "apply", and "bind" functions lazily.
605 |       boolean isCall = "call".equals(name);
606 |       boolean isBind = "bind".equals(name);
607 |       if (isCall || isBind) {
608 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name, getCallOrBindSignature(isCall), source);
609 |       } else if ("apply".equals(name)) {
610 |         // Define the "apply" function lazily.
611 |         FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
612 | 
613 |         // ECMA-262 says that apply's second argument must be an Array
614 |         // or an arguments object. We don't model the arguments object,
615 |         // so let's just be forgiving for now.
616 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Model the Arguments object.
617 |         builder.addOptionalParams(
618 |             registry.createNullableType(getTypeOfThis()),
619 |             registry.createNullableType(
620 |                 registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE)));
621 | 
622 |         defineDeclaredProperty(name,
623 |             new FunctionBuilder(registry)
624 |             .withParams(builder)
625 |             .withReturnType(getReturnType())
626 |             .withTemplateKeys(getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys())
627 |             .build(),
628 |             source);
629 |       }
630 |     }
631 | 
632 |     return super.getPropertyType(name);
633 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-101
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       boolean visitedFunction = false;
56 |       for (Node current = node;
57 |            current != null;
58 |            current = current.getParent()) {
59 |         int type = current.getType();
60 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
61 | 
62 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
63 |           info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
64 |           visitedFunction = true;
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         } else if (current.isVar() || current.isAssign()) {
68 |           // There's one edge case we're worried about:
69 |           // if the warning points to an assigment to a function, we
70 |           // want the suppressions on that function to apply.
71 |           // It's OK if we double-count some cases.
72 |           Node rhs = NodeUtil.getRValueOfLValue(current.getFirstChild());
73 |           if (rhs != null) {
74 |             if (rhs.isCast()) {
75 |               rhs = rhs.getFirstChild();
76 |             }
77 | 
78 |             if (rhs.isFunction() && !visitedFunction) {
79 |               info = NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(current);
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 | 
84 |         if (info != null) {
85 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
86 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
87 | 
88 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
89 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
90 |             if (guard != null) {
91 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
92 |               if (newLevel != null) {
93 |                 return newLevel;
94 |               }
95 |             }
96 |           }
97 |         }
98 |       }
99 |     }
100 |     return null;
101 |   }

===== M0407 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.findOverriddenFunction(ObjectType,String,TemplateTypeMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1014-1043
Documentation:
/**
     * Find the function that's being overridden on this type, if any.
     */
Method code:
1014 |     private FunctionType findOverriddenFunction(
1015 |         ObjectType ownerType, String propName, TemplateTypeMap typeMap) {
1016 |       FunctionType result = null;
1017 | 
1018 |       // First, check to see if the property is implemented
1019 |       // on a superclass.
1020 |       JSType propType = ownerType.getPropertyType(propName);
1021 |       if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
1022 |         result =  propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1023 |       } else {
1024 |         // If it's not, then check to see if it's implemented
1025 |         // on an implemented interface.
1026 |         for (ObjectType iface :
1027 |                  ownerType.getCtorImplementedInterfaces()) {
1028 |           propType = iface.getPropertyType(propName);
1029 |           if (propType != null && propType.isFunctionType()) {
1030 |             result = propType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1031 |             break;
1032 |           }
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       if (result != null && typeMap != null && !typeMap.isEmpty()) {
1037 |         result = result.visit(
1038 |             new TemplateTypeMapReplacer(typeRegistry, typeMap))
1039 |             .toMaybeFunctionType();
1040 |       }
1041 | 
1042 |       return result;
1043 |     }

===== M0410 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ModificationVisitor.caseFunctionType(FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ModificationVisitor.java
Original range: 87-138
Documentation:

Method code:
87 |   @Override
88 |   public JSType caseFunctionType(FunctionType type) {
89 |     if (isNativeFunctionType(type)) {
90 |       return type;
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     // TODO(johnlenz): remove this simplifying assumption...
94 |     if (!type.isOrdinaryFunction()) {
95 |       return type;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     boolean changed = false;
99 | 
100 |     JSType beforeThis = type.getTypeOfThis();
101 |     JSType afterThis = coerseToThisType(beforeThis.visit(this));
102 |     if (beforeThis != afterThis) {
103 |       changed = true;
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     JSType beforeReturn = type.getReturnType();
107 |     JSType afterReturn = beforeReturn.visit(this);
108 |     if (beforeReturn != afterReturn) {
109 |       changed = true;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(registry);
113 |     for (Node paramNode : type.getParameters()) {
114 |       JSType beforeParamType = paramNode.getJSType();
115 |       JSType afterParamType = beforeParamType.visit(this);
116 |       if (beforeParamType != afterParamType) {
117 |         changed = true;
118 |       }
119 |       if (paramNode.isOptionalArg()) {
120 |         paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(afterParamType);
121 |       } else if (paramNode.isVarArgs()) {
122 |         paramBuilder.addVarArgs(afterParamType);
123 |       } else {
124 |         paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(afterParamType);
125 |       }
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     if (changed) {
129 |       FunctionBuilder builder = new FunctionBuilder(registry);
130 |       builder.withParams(paramBuilder);
131 |       builder.withReturnType(afterReturn);
132 |       builder.withTypeOfThis(afterThis);
133 |       builder.withTemplateKeys(type.getTemplateTypeMap().getUnfilledTemplateKeys());
134 |       return builder.build();
135 |     }
136 | 
137 |     return type;
138 |   }

===== M0417 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 632-646
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

68 |   public JSType caseTemplateType(TemplateType type) {
69 |     if (replacements.hasTemplateKey(type)) {

===== M0435 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 295-354
Documentation:

Method code:
295 |   private void validateTypeAnnotations(JSDocInfo info, AstNode node) {
296 |     if (info.hasType()) {
297 |       boolean valid = false;
298 |       switch (node.getType()) {
299 |         // Casts are valid
300 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.LP:
301 |           valid = node instanceof ParenthesizedExpression;
302 |           break;
303 |         // Variable declarations are valid
304 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.VAR:
305 |           valid = true;
306 |           break;
307 |         // Function declarations are valid
308 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.FUNCTION:
309 |           FunctionNode fnNode = (FunctionNode) node;
310 |           valid = fnNode.getFunctionType() == FunctionNode.FUNCTION_STATEMENT;
311 |           break;
312 |         // Object literal properties, catch declarations and variable
313 |         // initializers are valid.
314 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NAME:
315 |           AstNode parent = node.getParent();
316 |           valid = parent instanceof ObjectProperty
317 |               || parent instanceof CatchClause
318 |               || parent instanceof FunctionNode
319 |               || (parent instanceof VariableInitializer &&
320 |                   node == ((VariableInitializer) parent).getTarget());
321 |           break;
322 |         // Object literal properties are valid
323 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GET:
324 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.SET:
325 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.NUMBER:
326 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.STRING:
327 |           valid = node.getParent() instanceof ObjectProperty;
328 |           break;
329 | 
330 |         // Property assignments are valid, if at the root of an expression.
331 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.ASSIGN:
332 |           if (node instanceof Assignment) {
333 |             valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent())
334 |                 && isPropAccess(((Assignment) node).getLeft());
335 |           }
336 |           break;
337 | 
338 |         // Property definitions are valid, if at the root of an expression.
339 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETPROP:
340 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.GETELEM:
341 |           valid = isExprStmt(node.getParent());
342 |           break;
343 | 
344 |         case com.google.javascript.rhino.head.Token.CALL:
345 |           valid = info.isDefine();
346 |           break;
347 |       }
348 |       if (!valid) {
349 |         errorReporter.warning(MISPLACED_TYPE_ANNOTATION,
350 |             sourceName,
351 |             node.getLineno(), "", 0);
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 |   }

===== M0438 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 746-788
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
746 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
747 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
748 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType,
749 |         classTemplateTypeNames);
750 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
751 | 
752 |     if (makesStructs) {
753 |       fnType.setStruct();
754 |     } else if (makesDicts) {
755 |       fnType.setDict();
756 |     }
757 |     if (existingType != null) {
758 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
759 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
760 |         FunctionType existingFn =
761 |             isInstanceObject ?
762 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
763 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
764 | 
765 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
766 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
767 |         }
768 | 
769 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
770 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, formatFnName(),
771 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
772 |         }
773 | 
774 |         return existingFn;
775 |       } else {
776 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
777 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
778 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
779 |       }
780 |     }
781 | 
782 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
783 | 
784 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
785 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
786 |     }
787 |     return fnType;
788 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processNewExpression(NewExpression)

Method code:
942 |     @Override
943 |     Node processNewExpression(NewExpression exprNode) {
944 |       Node node = newNode(
945 |           transformTokenType(exprNode.getType()),
946 |           transform(exprNode.getTarget()));
947 |       for (AstNode child : exprNode.getArguments()) {
948 |         node.addChildToBack(transform(child));
949 |       }
950 |       node.setLineno(exprNode.getLineno());
951 |       node.setCharno(position2charno(exprNode.getAbsolutePosition()));
952 |       maybeSetLengthFrom(node, exprNode);
953 |       return node;
954 |     }

504 |   private FlowScope traverseAssign(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
505 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
506 |     Node right = n.getLastChild();
507 |     scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
508 |

/**
     * Returns the type specified in a JSDoc annotation near a GETPROP or NAME.
     *
     * Extracts type information from either the {@code @type} tag or from
     * the {@code @return} and {@code @param} tags.
     */
Method code:
708 |     private JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(Node node, JSDocInfo info) {
709 |       JSType jsType = null;
710 |       if (info != null) {
711 |         if (info.hasType()) {
712 | 
713 |           ImmutableList<TemplateType> ownerTypeKeys = ImmutableList.of();
714 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(node);
715 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
716 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
717 |           if (ownerName != null) {
718 |             Var ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
719 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
720 |               ownerType = getPrototypeOwnerType(
721 |                   ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType()));

723 |                 ownerTypeKeys =
724 |                     ownerType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys();
725 |               }
726 |             }
727 |           }
728 | 
729 |           if (!ownerTypeKeys.isEmpty()) {
730 |             typeRegistry.setTemplateTypeNames(ownerTypeKeys);
731 |           }
732 | 
733 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
734 | 
735 |           if (!ownerTypeKeys.isEmpty()) {
736 |             typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeNames();
737 |           }
738 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
739 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();

/**
   * Create a new union type, with an alternate that has already been
   * parsed. The alternate may be null.
   */
Method code:
2160 |   private Node parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(JsDocToken token, Node alternate) {
2161 |     Node union = newNode(Token.PIPE);
2162 |     if (alternate != null) {
2163 |       union.addChildToBack(alternate);
2164 |     }
2165 | 
2166 |     Node expr = null;
2167 |     do {
2168 |       if (expr != null) {
2169 |         skipEOLs();
2170 |         token = next();
