2172 |             token == JsDocToken.PIPE || token == JsDocToken.COMMA);
2173 | 
2174 |         boolean isPipe = token == JsDocToken.PIPE;

2176 |           // We support double pipes for backwards compatibility.
2177 |           next();
2178 |         }

2180 |         token = next();
2181 |       }

2183 |       if (expr == null) {
2184 |         return null;

2189 |     } while (match(JsDocToken.PIPE, JsDocToken.COMMA));

===== M0472 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getDeclaredType(JSDocInfo,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1338-1391
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for a type declaration on a property assignment
     * (in an ASSIGN or an object literal key).
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param lValue The l-value node.
     * @param rValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1338 |     private JSType getDeclaredType(JSDocInfo info, Node lValue,
1339 |         @Nullable Node rValue) {
1340 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
1341 |         return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(lValue, info);
1342 |       } else if (rValue != null && rValue.isFunction() &&
1343 |           shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
1344 |               JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(rValue.getJSType()), info, lValue)) {
1345 |         return rValue.getJSType();
1346 |       } else if (info != null) {
1347 |         if (info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
1348 |           if (rValue != null && rValue.isObjectLit()) {
1349 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1350 |           } else {
1351 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1352 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1353 |           }
1354 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1355 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1356 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1357 |         }
1358 |       }
1359 | 
1360 |       // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1361 |       if (isConstantSymbol(info, lValue)) {
1362 |         if (rValue != null) {
1363 |           JSDocInfo rValueInfo = rValue.getJSDocInfo();
1364 |           if (rValueInfo != null && rValueInfo.hasType()) {
1365 |             // If rValue has a type-cast, we use the type in the type-cast.
1366 |             return rValueInfo.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1367 |           } else if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1368 |               && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1369 |             // If rValue's type was already computed during scope creation,
1370 |             // then we can safely use that.
1371 |             return rValue.getJSType();
1372 |           } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1373 |             // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1374 |             // var x = x || TYPE;
1375 |             // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1376 |             // reasons.
1377 |             Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1378 |             Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1379 |             boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1380 |                 && lValue.isName()
1381 |                 && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1382 |             if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1383 |                 && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1384 |               return secondClause.getJSType();
1385 |             }
1386 |           }
1387 |         }
1388 |       }
1389 | 
1390 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(lValue, info);
1391 |     }

===== M0485 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1164-1289
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1164 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1165 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1166 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1167 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1168 |     // already.
1169 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1170 |       return;
1171 |     }
1172 | 
1173 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1174 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1175 |         superClass.getInstanceType().hasProperty(propertyName);
1176 |     boolean superClassHasDeclaredProperty = superClass != null &&
1177 |         superClass.getInstanceType().isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1178 | 
1179 |     // For interface
1180 |     boolean superInterfaceHasProperty = false;
1181 |     boolean superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty = false;
1182 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1183 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1184 |         superInterfaceHasProperty =
1185 |             superInterfaceHasProperty ||
1186 |             interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1187 |         superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty =
1188 |             superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty ||
1189 |             interfaceType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propertyName);
1190 |       }
1191 |     }
1192 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1193 | 
1194 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1195 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1196 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1197 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1198 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1199 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1200 |           continue;
1201 |         }
1202 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1203 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1204 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1205 | 
1206 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1207 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1208 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty ||
1209 |             interfaceHasProperty;
1210 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1211 |             && !declaredOverride
1212 |             && interfaceHasProperty) {
1213 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1214 |           // property
1215 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1216 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1217 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1218 |         }
1219 |       }
1220 |     }
1221 | 
1222 |     if (!declaredOverride
1223 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1224 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1225 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1226 |       return;
1227 |     }
1228 | 
1229 |     ObjectType topInstanceType = superClassHasDeclaredProperty ?
1230 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1231 |     boolean declaredLocally =
1232 |         ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1233 |         (ctorType.getPrototype().hasOwnProperty(propertyName) ||
1234 |          ctorType.getInstanceType().hasOwnProperty(propertyName));
1235 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn()
1236 |         && !declaredOverride
1237 |         && superClassHasDeclaredProperty
1238 |         && declaredLocally) {
1239 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1240 |       // property
1241 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1242 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1243 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1244 |     }
1245 | 
1246 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1247 |     if (superClassHasDeclaredProperty) {
1248 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1249 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1250 |           superClass.getInstanceType().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1251 |       TemplateTypeMap ctorTypeMap =
1252 |           ctorType.getTypeOfThis().getTemplateTypeMap();
1253 |       if (!ctorTypeMap.isEmpty()) {
1254 |         superClassPropType = superClassPropType.visit(
1255 |             new TemplateTypeMapReplacer(typeRegistry, ctorTypeMap));
1256 |       }
1257 | 
1258 |       if (!propertyType.isSubtype(superClassPropType)) {
1259 |         compiler.report(
1260 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1261 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1262 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1263 |       }
1264 |     } else if (superInterfaceHasDeclaredProperty) {
1265 |       // there is an super interface property
1266 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1267 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1268 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1269 |               interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1270 |           if (!propertyType.isSubtype(superPropertyType)) {
1271 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1272 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1273 |             compiler.report(
1274 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1275 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1276 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1277 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1278 |           }
1279 |         }
1280 |       }
1281 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty
1282 |         && !superClassHasProperty
1283 |         && !superInterfaceHasProperty) {
1284 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1285 |       compiler.report(
1286 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1287 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1288 |     }
1289 |   }

   * Returns whether a node corresponds to a simple or a qualified name, such as
   * <code>x</code> or <code>a.b.c</code> or <code>this.a</code>.

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

/**
     * If a variable is assigned a function literal in the global scope,
     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).
     * There's only one exception to this rule:
     * if the return type is inferred, and we're in a local
     * scope, we should assume the whole function is inferred.
     */
Method code:
839 |     private boolean shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
840 |         FunctionType type, JSDocInfo info, Node lValue) {
841 |       if (info != null) {
842 |         return true;
843 |       }
844 |       if (lValue != null &&
845 |           NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(lValue)) {
846 |         return false;
847 |       }
848 |       return scope.isGlobal() || !type.isReturnTypeInferred();
849 |     }

===== M0516 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1193-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1193 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1194 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1195 |       return -1;
1196 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1198 |     } else {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1200 |     }
1201 |   }

234 |       Set<String> propertyNames = Sets.newTreeSet();
235 |       for (ObjectType current = this;
236 |            current != null && !current.isNativeObjectType() &&
237 |                propertyNames.size() <= MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES;
238 |            current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
239 |         propertyNames.addAll(current.getOwnPropertyNames());
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
243 |       sb.append("{");
244 | 
245 |       int i = 0;
246 |       for (String property : propertyNames) {
247 |         if (i > 0) {
248 |           sb.append(", ");
249 |         }
250 | 
251 |         sb.append(property);
252 |         sb.append(": ");
253 |         sb.append(getPropertyType(property).toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
254 | 
255 |         ++i;
256 |         if (!forAnnotations && i == MAX_PRETTY_PRINTED_PROPERTIES) {
257 |           sb.append(", ...");
258 |           break;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       sb.append("}");
263 | 
264 |       prettyPrint = true;
265 |       return sb.toString();
266 |     } else {
267 |       return forAnnotations ? "?" : "{...}";
268 |     }
269 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldType(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2298-2326
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldType := FieldName | FieldName ':' TypeExpression
   */
Method code:
2298 |   private Node parseFieldType(JsDocToken token) {
2299 |     Node fieldName = parseFieldName(token);
2300 | 
2301 |     if (fieldName == null) {
2302 |       return null;
2303 |     }
2304 | 
2305 |     skipEOLs();
2306 |     if (!match(JsDocToken.COLON)) {
2307 |       return fieldName;
2308 |     }
2309 | 
2310 |     // Move to the colon.
2311 |     next();
2312 | 
2313 |     // Move to the token after the colon and parse
2314 |     // the type expression.
2315 |     skipEOLs();
2316 |     Node typeExpression = parseTypeExpression(next());
2317 | 
2318 |     if (typeExpression == null) {
2319 |       return null;
2320 |     }
2321 | 
2322 |     Node fieldType = newNode(Token.COLON);
2323 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(fieldName);
2324 |     fieldType.addChildToBack(typeExpression);
2325 |     return fieldType;
2326 |   }

===== M0534 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 159-205
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
159 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
160 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
161 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
162 |       case Token.BLOCK:
163 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
164 |       case Token.TRY:
165 |         return true;
166 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
167 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
168 |         // bleeds into the local scope and parameters are assigned
169 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
170 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
171 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
172 |         // control is going into the function's body, represented by the second
173 |         // child.
174 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
175 |       case Token.WHILE:
176 |       case Token.DO:
177 |       case Token.IF:
178 |         // These control structures are represented by a node that holds the
179 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
180 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.FOR:
183 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structures in that
184 |         // it has an initialization and an increment statement. Those
185 |         // two statements have corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
186 |         // The FOR node only represents the condition check for each iteration.
187 |         // That way the following:
188 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
189 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
190 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
191 |           // TODO(user): Investigate how we should handle the case where
192 |           // we have a very complex expression inside the FOR-IN header.
193 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
194 |         } else {
195 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
196 |         }
197 |       case Token.SWITCH:
198 |       case Token.CASE:
199 |       case Token.CATCH:
200 |       case Token.WITH:
201 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
202 |       default:
203 |         return false;
204 |     }
205 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitReturn(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1871-1899
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a RETURN node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1871 |   private void visitReturn(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1872 |     JSType jsType = getJSType(t.getEnclosingFunction());
1873 | 
1874 |     if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1875 |       FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1876 | 
1877 |       JSType returnType = functionType.getReturnType();
1878 | 
1879 |       // if no return type is specified, undefined must be returned
1880 |       // (it's a void function)
1881 |       if (returnType == null) {
1882 |         returnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1883 |       }
1884 | 
1885 |       // fetching the returned value's type
1886 |       Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
1887 |       JSType actualReturnType;
1888 |       if (valueNode == null) {
1889 |         actualReturnType = getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1890 |         valueNode = n;
1891 |       } else {
1892 |         actualReturnType = getJSType(valueNode);
1893 |       }
1894 | 
1895 |       // verifying
1896 |       validator.expectCanAssignTo(t, valueNode, actualReturnType, returnType,
1897 |           "inconsistent return type");
1898 |     }
1899 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 307-462
Documentation:

Method code:
307 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
308 |     switch (n.getType()) {
309 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
310 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
311 |         break;
312 | 
313 |       case Token.NAME:
314 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
315 |         break;
316 | 
317 |       case Token.GETPROP:
318 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
319 |         break;
320 | 
321 |       case Token.AND:
322 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
323 |             .createChildFlowScope();
324 |         break;
325 | 
326 |       case Token.OR:
327 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
328 |             .createChildFlowScope();
329 |         break;
330 | 
331 |       case Token.HOOK:
332 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
333 |         break;
334 | 
335 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
336 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
337 |         break;
338 | 
339 |       case Token.CALL:
340 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
341 |         break;
342 | 
343 |       case Token.NEW:
344 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
345 |         break;
346 | 
347 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
348 |       case Token.ADD:
349 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.POS:
353 |       case Token.NEG:
354 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
355 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
356 |         break;
357 | 
358 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
359 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
360 |         break;
361 | 
362 |       case Token.THIS:
363 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
364 |         break;
365 | 
366 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
367 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
368 |       case Token.LSH:
369 |       case Token.RSH:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
371 |       case Token.URSH:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
373 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
374 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
375 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
376 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
377 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
378 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
379 |       case Token.DIV:
380 |       case Token.MOD:
381 |       case Token.BITAND:
382 |       case Token.BITXOR:
383 |       case Token.BITOR:
384 |       case Token.MUL:
385 |       case Token.SUB:
386 |       case Token.DEC:
387 |       case Token.INC:
388 |       case Token.BITNOT:
389 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
390 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
391 |         break;
392 | 
393 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
394 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
395 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
396 |         break;
397 | 
398 |       case Token.COMMA:
399 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
400 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
404 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
405 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
406 |         break;
407 | 
408 |       case Token.DELPROP:
409 |       case Token.LT:
410 |       case Token.LE:
411 |       case Token.GT:
412 |       case Token.GE:
413 |       case Token.NOT:
414 |       case Token.EQ:
415 |       case Token.NE:
416 |       case Token.SHEQ:
417 |       case Token.SHNE:
418 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
419 |       case Token.IN:
420 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
421 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
422 |         break;
423 | 
424 |       case Token.GETELEM:
425 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
426 |         break;
427 | 
428 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
429 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
430 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isGetProp()) {
431 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
432 |         }
433 |         break;
434 | 
435 |       case Token.SWITCH:
436 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
437 |         break;
438 | 
439 |       case Token.RETURN:
440 |         scope = traverseReturn(n, scope);
441 |         break;
442 | 
443 |       case Token.VAR:
444 |       case Token.THROW:
445 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
446 |         break;
447 | 
448 |       case Token.CATCH:
449 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       case Token.CAST:
453 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
454 |         JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
455 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
456 |           n.setJSType(info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry));
457 |         }
458 |         break;
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     return scope;
462 |   }

Documentation:
/** Get the name of the given l-value node. */
Method code:
3170 |   static String getBestLValueName(@Nullable Node lValue) {
3171 |     if (lValue == null || lValue.getParent() == null) {
3172 |       return null;
3173 |     }
3174 |     if (isObjectLitKey(lValue)) {
3175 |       Node owner = getBestLValue(lValue.getParent());
3176 |       if (owner != null) {
3177 |         String ownerName = getBestLValueName(owner);
3178 |         if (ownerName != null) {
3179 |           return ownerName + "." + getObjectLitKeyName(lValue);
3180 |         }
3181 |       }
3182 |       return null;
3183 |     }
3184 |     return lValue.getQualifiedName();
3185 |   }

===== M0553 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1033-1062
Documentation:

Method code:
1033 |   private void checkPropertyInheritanceOnGetpropAssign(
1034 |       NodeTraversal t, Node assign, Node object, String property,
1035 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1036 |     // Inheritance checks for prototype properties.
1037 |     //
1038 |     // TODO(nicksantos): This isn't the right place to do this check. We
1039 |     // really want to do this when we're looking at the constructor.
1040 |     // We'd find all its properties and make sure they followed inheritance
1041 |     // rules, like we currently do for @implements to make sure
1042 |     // all the methods are implemented.
1043 |     //
1044 |     // As-is, this misses many other ways to override a property.
1045 |     //
1046 |     // object.prototype.property = ...;
1047 |     if (object.isGetProp()) {
1048 |       Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
1049 |       String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
1050 | 
1051 |       if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
1052 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
1053 |         if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
1054 |           FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1055 |           if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
1056 |             checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1057 |                 t, assign, functionType, property, info, propertyType);
1058 |           }
1059 |         }
1060 |       }
1061 |     }
1062 |   }

/**
   * Declares a variable.

   * @param nameNode the NAME node declaring the variable

   * @param input the input in which this variable is defined.

493 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place

/**
   * Counts the given node in the typed statistics.
   * @param n a node that should be typed
   */
Method code:
890 |   private void doPercentTypedAccounting(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
891 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();

893 |       nullCount++;
894 |     } else if (type.isUnknownType()) {

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1828-1862
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1828 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1829 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1830 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1831 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1832 | 
1833 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1834 |     int ordinal = 0;
1835 |     Node parameter = null;
1836 |     Node argument = null;
1837 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1838 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1839 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1840 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1841 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1842 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1843 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1844 |       }
1845 |       argument = arguments.next();
1846 |       ordinal++;
1847 | 
1848 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1849 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1850 |     }
1851 | 
1852 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1853 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1854 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1855 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1856 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1857 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1858 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1859 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1860 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1861 |     }
1862 |   }

===== M0600 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.matchConstraint(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 427-452
Documentation:

Method code:
427 |   @Override
428 |   public void matchConstraint(JSType constraint) {
429 |     // We only want to match constraints on anonymous types.
430 |     if (hasReferenceName()) {
431 |       return;
432 |     }
433 | 
434 |     // Handle the case where the constraint object is a record type.
435 |     //
436 |     // param constraint {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
437 |     // function f(constraint) {}
438 |     // f({});
439 |     //
440 |     // We want to modify the object literal to match the constraint, by
441 |     // taking any each property on the record and trying to match
442 |     // properties on this object.
443 |     if (constraint.isRecordType()) {
444 |       matchRecordTypeConstraint(constraint.toObjectType());
445 |     } else if (constraint.isUnionType()) {
446 |       for (JSType alt : constraint.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
447 |         if (alt.isRecordType()) {
448 |           matchRecordTypeConstraint(alt.toObjectType());
449 |         }
450 |       }
451 |     }
452 |   }

===== M0611 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 639-666
Documentation:

Method code:
639 |   private void handleReturn(Node node) {
640 |     Node lastJump = null;
641 |     for (Iterator<Node> iter = exceptionHandler.iterator(); iter.hasNext();) {
642 |       Node curHandler = iter.next();
643 |       if (curHandler.isFunction()) {
644 |         break;
645 |       }
646 |       if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(curHandler)) {
647 |         if (lastJump == null) {
648 |           createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, curHandler.getLastChild());
649 |         } else {
650 |           finallyMap.put(lastJump,
651 |               computeFallThrough(curHandler.getLastChild()));
652 |         }
653 |         lastJump = curHandler;
654 |       }
655 |     }
656 | 
657 |     if (node.hasChildren()) {
658 |       connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(node, node.getFirstChild());
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (lastJump == null) {
662 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, null);
663 |     } else {
664 |       finallyMap.put(lastJump, null);
665 |     }
666 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()

Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub declarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1765 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1766 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1767 |         Node n = stub.node;
1768 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1769 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1770 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1771 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1772 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1773 | 
1774 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1775 |           continue;
1776 |         }
1777 | 
1778 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1779 |         // in the type registry.
1780 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1781 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1782 | 
1783 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1784 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1785 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1786 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1787 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1788 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1789 |         } else {
1790 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1791 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1792 |         }
1793 |       }
1794 |     }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 335-389
Documentation:

Method code:
335 |   protected int getChar() {
336 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
337 |       cursor++;
338 |       --ungetCursor;
339 |       if (charno == -1) {
340 |         charno = getOffset();
341 |       }
342 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
343 |     }
344 | 
345 |     for (;;) {
346 |       int c;
347 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
348 |         hitEOF = true;
349 |         if (charno == -1) {
350 |           charno = getOffset();
351 |         }
352 |         return EOF_CHAR;
353 |       }
354 |       cursor++;
355 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
356 | 
357 | 
358 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
359 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
360 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
361 |           continue;
362 |         }
363 |         lineEndChar = -1;
364 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
365 |         lineno++;
366 |       }
367 | 
368 |       if (c <= 127) {
369 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
370 |           lineEndChar = c;
371 |           c = '\n';
372 |         }
373 |       } else {
374 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
375 |           continue;
376 |         }
377 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
378 |           lineEndChar = c;
379 |           c = '\n';
380 |         }
381 |       }
382 | 
383 |       if (charno == -1) {
384 |         charno = getOffset();
385 |       }
386 | 
387 |       return c;
388 |     }
389 |   }

===== M0627 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 524-584
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   * @return The variable we end up with. Most of the time, this will just
   *     be {@code var}, but in some rare cases we will need to declare
   *     a new var with new source info.
   */
Method code:
524 |   Var expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
525 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
526 |     Var newVar = var;
527 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
528 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
529 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n)) {
530 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
531 |       if (info == null) {
532 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
533 |       }
534 |       allowDupe =
535 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
536 |     }
537 | 
538 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
539 | 
540 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
541 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
542 |     // compilation process.
543 |     if (varType != null &&
544 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
545 |         newType != null &&
546 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
547 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
548 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
549 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
550 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
551 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
552 |       if (var.input == null) {
553 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
554 |         s.undeclare(var);
555 |         newVar = s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
556 | 
557 |         n.setJSType(varType);
558 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
559 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
560 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
561 |           }
562 |         } else {
563 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
564 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
565 |         }
566 |       } else {
567 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
568 |         // match the original type.
569 |         //
570 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
571 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
572 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
573 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
574 |             !newType.isEquivalentTo(varType)) {
575 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
576 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
577 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
578 |               varType.toString()));
579 |         }
580 |       }
581 |     }
582 | 
583 |     return newVar;
584 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 548-561
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
548 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
549 |       Node ret;
550 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
551 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name) n);
552 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
553 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral) n);
554 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
555 |       } else {
556 |         ret = transform(n);
557 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
558 |       }
559 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
560 |       return ret;
561 |     }

===== M0635 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0638 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 1022-1062
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
1022 |   @Override
1023 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1024 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 | 
1028 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
1029 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
1030 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
1031 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
1032 |         return true;
1033 |       }
1034 |       if (isInterface()) {
1035 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
1036 |         return false;
1037 |       }
1038 | 
1039 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
1040 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
1041 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
1042 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
1043 |       // this as an error. Let's punt on all this for now.
1044 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
1045 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
1046 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
1047 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
1048 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
1049 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
1050 |         (other.typeOfThis.toObjectType() != null &&
1051 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor() != null &&
1052 |              other.typeOfThis.toObjectType().getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
1053 | 
1054 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
1055 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
1056 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
1057 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
1058 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
1059 |     }
1060 | 
1061 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
1062 |   }

922 |         case PRIVATE:
923 |         case PROTECTED:
924 |         case PUBLIC:
925 |         case THIS:
926 |         case TYPE:
927 |         case TYPEDEF:
928 |           lineno = stream.getLineno();
929 |           charno = stream.getCharno();
930 | 
931 |           Node typeNode = null;
932 |           boolean hasType = lookAheadForTypeAnnotation();
933 |           boolean isAlternateTypeAnnotation =
934 |               (annotation == Annotation.PRIVATE ||
935 |                annotation == Annotation.PROTECTED ||
936 |                annotation == Annotation.PUBLIC ||
937 |                annotation == Annotation.CONSTANT);
938 |           boolean canSkipTypeAnnotation =
939 |               (isAlternateTypeAnnotation ||
940 |                annotation == Annotation.RETURN);
941 |           type = null;
942 |           if (hasType || !canSkipTypeAnnotation) {
943 |             skipEOLs();
944 |             token = next();
945 |             typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token);
946 | 
947 |             if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
948 |               typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
949 |             }
950 |             type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
951 |           }
952 | 
953 |           // The error was reported during recursive descent
954 |           // recovering parsing
955 |           boolean hasError = type == null && !canSkipTypeAnnotation;
956 |           if (!hasError) {
957 |             // Record types for @type.
958 |             // If the @private, @protected, or @public annotations
959 |             // have a type attached, pretend that they actually wrote:
960 |             // @type {type}\n@private
961 |             // This will have some weird behavior in some cases
962 |             // (for example, @private can now be used as a type-cast),
963 |             // but should be mostly OK.
964 |             if ((type != null && isAlternateTypeAnnotation)
965 |                 || annotation == Annotation.TYPE) {
966 |               if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
967 |                 parser.addTypeWarning(
968 |                     "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
969 |               }
970 |             }
971 | 
972 |             switch (annotation) {
973 |               case CONSTANT:
974 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
975 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
976 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
977 |                 }
978 |                 break;
979 | 
980 |               case DEFINE:
981 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
982 |                   parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
983 |                       lineno, charno);
984 |                 }
985 |                 return recordDescription(token);
986 | 
987 |               case PRIVATE:
988 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
989 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
990 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
991 |                       lineno, charno);
992 |                 }
993 |                 return recordDescription(token);
994 | 
995 |               case PROTECTED:
996 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
997 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
998 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
999 |                       lineno, charno);
1000 |                 }
1001 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1002 | 
1003 |               case PUBLIC:
1004 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
1005 |                   parser.addParserWarning(
1006 |                       "msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
1007 |                       lineno, charno);
1008 |                 }
1009 |                 return recordDescription(token);
1010 | 
1011 |               case RETURN:
1012 |                 if (type == null) {
1013 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(newNode(Token.QMARK));
1014 |                 }
1015 | 
1016 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
1017 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1018 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1019 |                   break;
1020 |                 }
1021 | 
1022 |                 // Find the return's description (if applicable).
1023 |                 if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
1024 |                   ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
1025 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
1026 | 
1027 |                   String returnDescription =
1028 |                       returnDescriptionInfo.string;
1029 | 
1030 |                   if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
1031 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
1032 |                         returnDescription);
1033 |                   }
1034 | 
1035 |                   token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
1036 |                 } else {
1037 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
1038 |                 }
1039 |                 return token;
1040 | 
1041 |               case THIS:
1042 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
1043 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1044 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1045 |                 }
1046 |                 break;
1047 | 
1048 |               case TYPEDEF:
1049 |                 if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
1050 |                   parser.addTypeWarning(
1051 |                       "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
1052 |                 }
1053 |                 break;
1054 |             }
1055 |           }
1056 | 
1057 |           return eatTokensUntilEOL();
1058 |       }
1059 |     }
1060 | 
1061 |     return next();
1062 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException

Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);

125 |

127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 |

131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {

134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {

===== M0655 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 964-971
Documentation:
/**
   * We only do forward type inference. We do not do full backwards
   * type inference.
   *
   * In other words, if we have,
   * <code>
   * var x = f();
   * g(x);
   * </code>
   * a forward type-inference engine would try to figure out the type
   * of "x" from the return type of "f". A backwards type-inference engine
   * would try to figure out the type of "x" from the parameter type of "g".
   *
   * However, there are a few special syntactic forms where we do some
   * some half-assed backwards type-inference, because programmers
   * expect it in this day and age. To take an example from Java,
   * <code>
   * List<String> x = Lists.newArrayList();
   * </code>
   * The Java compiler will be able to infer the generic type of the List
   * returned by newArrayList().
   *
   * In much the same way, we do some special-case backwards inference for
   * JS. Those cases are enumerated here.
   */
Method code:
964 |   private void backwardsInferenceFromCallSite(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
965 |     boolean updatedFnType = inferTemplatedTypesForCall(n, fnType);
966 |     if (updatedFnType) {
967 |       fnType = n.getFirstChild().getJSType().toMaybeFunctionType();
968 |     }
969 |     updateTypeOfParameters(n, fnType);
970 |     updateBind(n);
971 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
308 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1288 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1289 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);

1291 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1292 | 
1293 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1294 |     recordTypeNode(lineno, startCharno, typeNode, true);
1295 |     return typeNode;
1296 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferTemplateTypeName(JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 587-615
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the template type from the doc info.
   */
Method code:
587 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferTemplateTypeName(
588 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info, JSType ownerType) {
589 |     // NOTE: these template type names may override a list
590 |     // of inherited ones from an overridden function.
591 |     if (info != null &&  !info.getTemplateTypeNames().isEmpty()) {
592 |       ImmutableList.Builder<TemplateType> builder = ImmutableList.builder();
593 |       for (String key : info.getTemplateTypeNames()) {
594 |         builder.add(typeRegistry.createTemplateType(key));
595 |       }

597 |     }
598 | 
599 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> keys = templateTypeNames;

===== M0685 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

/**
   * TypeExpressionList := TopLevelTypeExpression
   *     | TopLevelTypeExpression ',' TypeExpressionList
   */
Method code:
1815 |   private Node parseTypeExpressionList(JsDocToken token) {
1816 |     Node typeExpr = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1817 |     if (typeExpr == null) {
1818 |       return null;
1819 |     }
1820 |     Node typeList = IR.block();
1821 |     typeList.addChildToBack(typeExpr);
1822 |     while (match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
1823 |       next();
1824 |       skipEOLs();
1825 |       typeExpr = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(next());
1826 |       if (typeExpr == null) {
1827 |         return null;
1828 |       }
1829 |       typeList.addChildToBack(typeExpr);
1830 |     }
1831 |     return typeList;
1832 |   }

===== M0701 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.RecordType.isSubtype(ObjectType,RecordType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/RecordType.java
Original range: 233-274
Documentation:
/** Determines if typeA is a subtype of typeB */
Method code:
233 |   static boolean isSubtype(ObjectType typeA, RecordType typeB) {
234 |     // typeA is a subtype of record type typeB iff:
235 |     // 1) typeA has all the properties declared in typeB.
236 |     // 2) And for each property of typeB,
237 |     //    2a) if the property of typeA is declared, it must be equal
238 |     //        to the type of the property of typeB,
239 |     //    2b) otherwise, it must be a subtype of the property of typeB.
240 |     //
241 |     // To figure out why this is true, consider the following pseudo-code:
242 |     // /** @type {{a: (Object,null)}} */ var x;
243 |     // /** @type {{a: !Object}} */ var y;
244 |     // var z = {a: {}};
245 |     // x.a = null;
246 |     //
247 |     // y cannot be assigned to x, because line 4 would violate y's declared
248 |     // properties. But z can be assigned to x. Even though z and y are the
249 |     // same type, the properties of z are inferred--and so an assignment
250 |     // to the property of z would not violate any restrictions on it.
251 |     for (String property : typeB.getOwnPropertyNames()) {
252 |       if (!typeA.hasProperty(property)) {
253 |         return false;
254 |       }
255 | 
256 |       JSType propA = typeA.getPropertyType(property);
257 |       JSType propB = typeB.getPropertyType(property);
258 |       if (typeA.isPropertyTypeDeclared(property)) {
259 |         // If one declared property isn't invariant,
260 |         // then the whole record isn't covariant.
261 |         if (!propA.isInvariant(propB)) {
262 |           return false;
263 |         }
264 |       } else {
265 |         // If one inferred property isn't a subtype,
266 |         // then the whole record isn't covariant.
267 |         if (!propA.isSubtype(propB)) {
268 |           return false;
269 |         }
270 |       }
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     return true;
274 |   }

81 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
82 |     int startErrorCount = compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount();
83 |     try {
84 |       ParserRunner.ParseResult result = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
85 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
86 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
87 |           logger_);
88 |       root = result.ast;
89 |       compiler.setOldParseTree(sourceFile.getName(), result.oldAst);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }
94 | 
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null ||
97 |         // Most passes try to report as many errors as possible,
98 |         // so there may already be errors. We only care if there were
99 |         // errors in the code we just parsed.
100 |         (compiler.getErrorManager().getErrorCount() > startErrorCount && !compiler.isIdeMode())) {
101 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
102 |       root = IR.script();
103 |     } else {
104 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
105 |     }
106 | 
107 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
108 |     // the source file and module.
109 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
110 |   }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 484-523
Documentation:

Method code:
484 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
485 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
486 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
487 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
488 |         parent.isTry() &&
489 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
490 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
491 |       return;
492 |     }
493 | 
494 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
495 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
496 | 
497 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
498 |     // function (unless it is called)
499 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
500 |       child = child.getNext();
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     if (child != null) {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
505 |     } else {
506 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
507 |     }
508 | 
509 |     // Synthetic blocks
510 |     if (parent != null) {
511 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
512 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
513 |         case Token.CASE:
514 |         case Token.TRY:
515 |           break;
516 |         default:
517 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
518 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
519 |           }
520 |           break;
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |   }

===== M0717 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 514-571
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
516 |       inputId = t.getInputId();
517 |       attachLiteralTypes(n);
518 | 
519 |       switch (n.getType()) {
520 |         case Token.CALL:
521 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n);
522 |           checkForCallingConventionDefiningCalls(n, delegateCallingConventions);
523 |           break;
524 | 
525 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
526 |           if (t.getInput() == null || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
527 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
528 |           }
529 | 
530 |           // Hoisted functions are handled during pre-traversal.
531 |           if (!NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
532 |             defineFunctionLiteral(n);
533 |           }
534 |           break;
535 | 
536 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
537 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
538 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
539 |           if (firstChild.isGetProp() &&
540 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
541 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
542 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
543 |           }
544 |           break;
545 | 
546 |         case Token.CATCH:
547 |           defineCatch(n);
548 |           break;
549 | 
550 |         case Token.VAR:
551 |           defineVar(n);
552 |           break;
553 | 
554 |         case Token.GETPROP:
555 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
556 |           if (parent.isExprResult() &&
557 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
558 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
559 |           }
560 |           break;
561 |       }
562 | 
563 |       // Analyze any @lends object literals in this statement.
564 |       if (n.getParent() != null && NodeUtil.isStatement(n) &&
565 |           lentObjectLiterals != null) {
566 |         for (Node objLit : lentObjectLiterals) {
567 |           defineObjectLiteral(objLit);
568 |         }
569 |         lentObjectLiterals.clear();
570 |       }
571 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.toStringHelper(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 941-981
Documentation:
/**
   * Informally, a function is represented by
   * {@code function (params): returnType} where the {@code params} is a comma
   * separated list of types, the first one being a special
   * {@code this:T} if the function expects a known type for {@code this}.
   */
Method code:
941 |   @Override
942 |   String toStringHelper(boolean forAnnotations) {
943 |     if (!isPrettyPrint() ||
944 |         this == registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_INSTANCE_TYPE)) {
945 |       return "Function";
946 |     }
947 | 
948 |     setPrettyPrint(false);
949 | 
950 |     StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder(32);
951 |     b.append("function (");
952 |     int paramNum = call.parameters.getChildCount();
953 |     boolean hasKnownTypeOfThis = !(typeOfThis instanceof UnknownType);
954 |     if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
955 |       if (isConstructor()) {
956 |         b.append("new:");
957 |       } else {
958 |         b.append("this:");
959 |       }
960 |       b.append(typeOfThis.toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
961 |     }
962 |     if (paramNum > 0) {
963 |       if (hasKnownTypeOfThis) {
964 |         b.append(", ");
965 |       }
966 |       Node p = call.parameters.getFirstChild();
967 |       appendArgString(b, p, forAnnotations);
968 | 
969 |       p = p.getNext();
970 |       while (p != null) {
971 |         b.append(", ");
972 |         appendArgString(b, p, forAnnotations);
973 |         p = p.getNext();
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 |     b.append("): ");
977 |     b.append(call.returnType.toStringHelper(forAnnotations));
978 | 
979 |     setPrettyPrint(true);
980 |     return b.toString();
981 |   }

/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */

292 |   public int getMinArguments() {
293 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
294 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position

301 |         min = i;
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 |     return min;
305 |   }

===== M0741 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 885-921
Documentation:

Method code:
885 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
886 |       Node callNode) {
887 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
888 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
889 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
890 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
891 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
892 |       return scope;
893 |     }
894 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
895 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
896 |       return scope;
897 |     }
898 |     JSType assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType(
899 |         callNode, registry);
900 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
901 | 
902 |     JSType narrowed;
903 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
904 |     if (assertedType == null) {
905 |       // Handle arbitrary expressions within the assert.
906 |       scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
907 |           assertedNode, scope, true);
908 |       // Build the result of the assertExpression
909 |       narrowed = getJSType(assertedNode).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
910 |     } else {
911 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
912 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
913 |       narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(assertedType);
914 |       if (assertedNodeName != null && type.differsFrom(narrowed)) {
915 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
916 |       }
917 |     }
918 | 
919 |     callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
920 |     return scope;
921 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Suppose X is an object with inferred properties.
   * Suppose also that X is used in a way where it would only type-check
   * correctly if some of those properties are widened.
   * Then we should be polite and automatically widen X's properties for him.
   *
   * For a concrete example, consider:
   * param x {{prop: (number|undefined)}}
   * function f(x) {}
   * f({});
   *
   * If we give the anonymous object an inferred property of (number|undefined),
   * then this code will type-check appropriately.
   */
Method code:
1296 |   private static void inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(
1297 |       JSType type, JSType constraint) {
1298 |     if (type == null || constraint == null) {
1299 |       return;
1300 |     }
1301 | 
1302 |     type.matchConstraint(constraint);
1303 |   }

===== M0772 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 570-642
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
570 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
571 |     if (this == that) {
572 |       return true;
573 |     }
574 | 
575 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
576 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
577 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
578 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
579 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
580 |         // with everyone.
581 |         return true;
582 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
583 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
584 |         // both unknown.
585 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
586 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
587 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
588 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
589 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
590 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
591 |         return false;
592 |       }
593 |     }
594 | 
595 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
596 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
597 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
598 |     }
599 | 
600 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
601 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
602 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
603 |     }
604 | 
605 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
606 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
607 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
608 |     }
609 | 
610 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
611 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
612 |       return false;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
616 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
617 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     if (isTemplateType() && that.isTemplateType()) {
621 |       return this == that;
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     // Unbox other proxies.
625 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
626 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
627 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
628 |               that, eqMethod);
629 |     }
630 | 
631 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
632 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
633 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
634 |           eqMethod);
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
638 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
639 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
640 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
641 |     return this == that;
642 |   }
