===== M1052 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-222
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
182 |         return n.getString();
183 | 
184 |       case Token.NAME:
185 |         String name = n.getString();
186 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
187 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
188 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
189 |           return name;
190 |         }
191 |         break;
192 | 
193 |       case Token.NUMBER:
194 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
195 | 
196 |       case Token.FALSE:
197 |         return "false";
198 | 
199 |       case Token.TRUE:
200 |         return "true";
201 | 
202 |       case Token.NULL:
203 |         return "null";
204 | 
205 |       case Token.VOID:
206 |         return "undefined";
207 | 
208 |       case Token.NOT:
209 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
210 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
211 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
212 |         }
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
216 |         return arrayToString(n);
217 | 
218 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
219 |         return "[object Object]";
220 |     }
221 |     return null;
222 |   }

===== M1066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1429-1457
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1429 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1430 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1431 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1432 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1433 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1434 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1435 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1436 |     if (propType.isEquivalentTo(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1437 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1438 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1439 |       if (objectType != null) {
1440 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1441 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1442 |         // faster in most cases).
1443 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1444 |             objectType.isEquivalentTo(
1445 |                 typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1446 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1447 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1448 |           } else {
1449 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1450 |           }
1451 |         }
1452 | 
1453 |       } else {
1454 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1455 |       }
1456 |     }
1457 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 483-553
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
483 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
484 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
485 |     if (argsParent == null) {
486 |       if (info == null) {
487 |         return this;
488 |       } else {
489 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
490 |       }
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     // arguments
494 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
495 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
496 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
497 |     }
498 | 
499 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
500 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
501 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
502 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
503 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
504 |     boolean isVarArgs = false;
505 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
506 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
507 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
508 | 
509 |       // type from JSDocInfo
510 |       JSType parameterType = null;
511 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
512 |       isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
513 | 
514 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
515 |         parameterType =
516 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
517 |       } else if (arg.getJSDocInfo() != null && arg.getJSDocInfo().hasType()) {
518 |         parameterType =
519 |             arg.getJSDocInfo().getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
520 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
521 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
522 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
523 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
524 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
525 |       } else {
526 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
527 |       }
528 | 
529 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
530 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
531 |           isOptionalParam,
532 |           isVarArgs);
533 | 
534 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
535 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     // Copy over any old parameters that aren't in the param list.
540 |     if (!isVarArgs) {
541 |       while (oldParameterType != null && !isVarArgs) {
542 |         builder.newParameterFromNode(oldParameterType);
543 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
544 |       }
545 |     }
546 | 
547 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
548 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, formatFnName());
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
552 |     return this;
553 |   }

298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     try {
304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
306 |

===== M1098 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 796-809
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
796 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
797 |     if (item == null) {
798 |       return null;
799 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
800 |       return item.getNext();
801 |     } else {
802 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
803 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
804 |         return item.chain(result);
805 |       } else {
806 |         return item;
807 |       }
808 |     }
809 |   }

===== M1104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitVar(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1591-1620
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1591 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1592 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1593 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1594 |     // merge to fix this.
1595 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1596 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1597 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1598 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1599 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1600 | 
1601 |       if (value != null) {
1602 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1603 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1604 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1605 | 
1606 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1607 |         if (info == null) {
1608 |           info = varInfo;
1609 |         }
1610 | 
1611 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1612 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1613 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1614 |         } else {
1615 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1616 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1617 |         }
1618 |       }
1619 |     }
1620 |   }

===== M1105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3087-3115
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3087 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3088 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3089 |     if (info == null) {
3090 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3091 |       if (parent == null) {
3092 |         return null;
3093 |       }
3094 | 
3095 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3096 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3097 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3098 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3099 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3100 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3101 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3102 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3103 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3104 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3105 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3106 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3107 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3108 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3109 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3110 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3111 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3112 |       }
3113 |     }
3114 |     return info;
3115 |   }

/**
   * Creates a JsDocInfoParser and parses the JsDoc string.
   *
   * Used both for handling individual JSDoc comments and for handling
   * file-level JSDoc comments (@fileoverview and @license).
   *
   * @param node The JsDoc Comment node to parse.
   * @param irNode
   * @return A JsDocInfoParser. Will contain either fileoverview JsDoc, or
   *     normal JsDoc, or no JsDoc (if the method parses to the wrong level).
   */
Method code:
464 |   private JsDocInfoParser createJsDocInfoParser(Comment node, Node irNode) {
465 |     String comment = node.getValue();

468 | 
469 |     // The JsDocInfoParser expects the comment without the initial '/**'.
470 |     int numOpeningChars = 3;

473 |           new JsDocTokenStream(comment.substring(numOpeningChars),

476 |           node,
477 |           irNode,
478 |           config,
479 |           errorReporter);

482 |     jsdocParser.parse();
483 |     return jsdocParser;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferTemplateTypesFromParameters(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1031-1055
Documentation:

Method code:
1031 |   private Map<TemplateType, JSType> inferTemplateTypesFromParameters(
1032 |       FunctionType fnType, Node call) {
1033 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeMap().getTemplateKeys().isEmpty()) {
1034 |       return Collections.emptyMap();
1035 |     }
1036 | 
1037 |     Map<TemplateType, JSType> resolvedTypes = Maps.newIdentityHashMap();
1038 | 
1039 |     Node callTarget = call.getFirstChild();
1040 |     if (NodeUtil.isGet(callTarget)) {
1041 |       Node obj = callTarget.getFirstChild();
1042 |       maybeResolveTemplatedType(
1043 |           fnType.getTypeOfThis(),
1044 |           getJSType(obj),
1045 |           resolvedTypes);
1046 |     }
1047 | 
1048 |     if (call.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1049 |       maybeResolveTemplateTypeFromNodes(
1050 |           fnType.getParameters(),
1051 |           call.getChildAtIndex(1).siblings(),
1052 |           resolvedTypes);
1053 |     }
1054 |     return resolvedTypes;
1055 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.inferTemplatedTypesForCall(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1183-1210
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
1183 |   private boolean inferTemplatedTypesForCall(
1184 |       Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
1185 |     final ImmutableList<TemplateType> keys = fnType.getTemplateTypeMap()
1186 |         .getTemplateKeys();
1187 |     if (keys.isEmpty()) {
1188 |       return false;
1189 |     }
1190 | 
1191 |     // Try to infer the template types
1192 |     Map<TemplateType, JSType> inferred = 
1193 |         inferTemplateTypesFromParameters(fnType, n);
1194 | 
1195 | 
1196 |     // Replace all template types. If we couldn't find a replacement, we
1197 |     // replace it with UNKNOWN.
1198 |     TemplateTypeReplacer replacer = new TemplateTypeReplacer(

1203 |         .toMaybeFunctionType();

1205 |

1207 |     n.setJSType(replacementFnType.getReturnType());
1208 | 
1209 |     return replacer.madeChanges;
1210 |   }

===== M1139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1575-1590
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1575 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1576 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1577 |       String name = getString();
1578 |       return name.isEmpty() ? null : name;
1579 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1580 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1581 |       if (left == null) {
1582 |         return null;
1583 |       }
1584 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1585 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1586 |       return "this";
1587 |     } else {
1588 |       return null;
1589 |     }
1590 |   }

===== M1148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 521-564
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
521 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
522 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
523 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
524 |     switch (left.getType()) {
525 |       case Token.NAME:
526 |         String varName = left.getString();
527 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
528 | 
529 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
530 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
531 |         // This has two purposes:
532 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
533 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
534 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
535 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
536 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
537 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
538 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
539 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
540 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
541 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
542 |         }
543 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
544 |             resultType : null);
545 | 
546 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
547 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
548 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
549 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
550 |         }
551 |         break;
552 |       case Token.GETPROP:
553 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
554 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
555 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
556 |               leftType == null ? unknownType : leftType,
557 |               resultType);
558 |         }
559 | 
560 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
561 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
562 |         break;
563 |     }
564 |   }

===== M1154 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1539-1645
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1539 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1540 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1541 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1542 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1543 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1544 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1545 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1546 | 
1547 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1548 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1549 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1550 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1551 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1552 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1553 |       //
1554 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1555 |       // the function has JsDoc or has not been declared before.
1556 |       //
1557 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1558 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1559 | 
1560 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1561 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(info, n, rhsValue);
1562 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1563 |         // Determining type for #5
1564 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1565 |       }
1566 | 
1567 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1568 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1569 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1570 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1571 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1572 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1573 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1574 |         if (qVar != null) {
1575 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1576 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1577 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1578 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1579 |           // the @extends tag.
1580 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1581 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1582 |               rhsValue != null &&
1583 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1584 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1585 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1586 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1587 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1588 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1589 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1590 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1591 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1592 |             return;
1593 |           }
1594 | 
1595 |           qVar.getScope().undeclare(qVar);
1596 |         }
1597 |       }
1598 | 
1599 |       if (valueType == null) {
1600 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1601 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1602 |               n,
1603 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1604 |               ownerName));
1605 |         }
1606 | 
1607 |         return;
1608 |       }
1609 | 
1610 |       boolean inferred = isQualifiedNameInferred(
1611 |           qName, n, info, rhsValue, valueType);
1612 |       if (!inferred) {
1613 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1614 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1615 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1616 |           // declared yet.
1617 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1618 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1619 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1620 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1621 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1622 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1623 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1624 |           }
1625 |         }
1626 | 
1627 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1628 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1629 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1630 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1631 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1632 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1633 |         FunctionType ownerFnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(ownerType);
1634 |         if (ownerFnType != null) {
1635 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerFnType.getTypeOfThis();
1636 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1637 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1638 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1639 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1640 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1641 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1642 |           }
1643 |         }
1644 |       }
1645 |     }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1793 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1794 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1795 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1796 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1797 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1798 |         next();
1799 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1800 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1801 |           next();
1802 |         }
1803 |         skipEOLs();
1804 |         token = next();
1805 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1806 |       }
1807 |     }
1808 |     return typeExpr;
1809 |   }

===== M1161 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

457 |     @Override
458 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
459 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
460 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
461 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
462 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());
463 | 
464 |       for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> predNode : predNodes) {
465 |         BranchedFlowState<L> predNodeState = predNode.getAnnotation();
466 | 
467 |         L in = predNodeState.out.get(
468 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
469 | 
470 |         values.add(in);
471 |       }
472 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
473 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
474 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
475 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
476 |       }
477 |     }

===== M1164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)

Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1893 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1894 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1895 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1896 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1897 |       skipEOLs();
1898 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1899 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1900 |       skipEOLs();
1901 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1902 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1903 |       skipEOLs();
1904 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1905 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1906 |       String string = stream.getString();
1907 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1908 |         skipEOLs();
1909 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1910 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1911 |         return newStringNode(string);
1912 |       } else {
1913 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1914 |       }
1915 |     }
1916 | 
1917 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1918 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1919 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
162 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
163 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
164 |     String methodName = null;

178 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {

===== M1188 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1771-1823
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1771 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1772 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1773 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1774 | 
1775 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1776 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1777 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1778 |       return;
1779 |     }
1780 | 
1781 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1782 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1783 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1784 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1785 | 
1786 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1787 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1788 |       if (functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1789 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1790 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1791 |       }
1792 | 
1793 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1794 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1795 |       // in an extern.
1796 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1797 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1798 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1799 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType())) {
1800 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1801 |       }
1802 | 
1803 |       // Functions with explicit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1804 |       // or GETELEM.
1805 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1806 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1807 |           !(functionType.getTypeOfThis().toObjectType() != null &&
1808 |           functionType.getTypeOfThis().toObjectType().isNativeObjectType()) &&
1809 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1810 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1811 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1812 |       }
1813 | 
1814 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1815 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1816 |     } else {
1817 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1818 |     }
1819 | 
1820 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects,
1821 |     // which is not supported by IE. Either say something about the return type
1822 |     // or warn about the non-portability of the call or both.
1823 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the property type of the property whose name is given. If the
   * underlying object does not have this property, the Unknown type is
   * returned to indicate that no information is available on this property.
   *
   * This gets overridden by FunctionType for lazily-resolved call() and
   * bind() functions.
   *
   * @return the property's type or {@link UnknownType}. This method never
   *         returns {@code null}.
   */
Method code:
421 |   public JSType getPropertyType(String propertyName) {
422 |     StaticSlot<JSType> slot = getSlot(propertyName);
423 |     if (slot == null) {
424 |       if (isNoResolvedType() || isCheckedUnknownType()) {
425 |         return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
426 |       } else if (isEmptyType()) {
427 |         return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE);
428 |       }
429 |       return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
430 |     }
431 |     return slot.getType();
432 |   }

===== M1212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2267-2293
Documentation:
/**
   * FieldTypeList := FieldType | FieldType ',' FieldTypeList
   */
Method code:
2267 |   private Node parseFieldTypeList(JsDocToken token) {
2268 |     Node fieldTypeList = newNode(Token.LB);
2269 | 
2270 |     do {
2271 |       Node fieldType = parseFieldType(token);
2272 | 
2273 |       if (fieldType == null) {
2274 |         return null;
2275 |       }
2276 | 
2277 |       fieldTypeList.addChildToBack(fieldType);
2278 | 
2279 |       skipEOLs();
2280 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.COMMA)) {
2281 |         break;
2282 |       }
2283 | 
2284 |       // Move to the comma token.
2285 |       next();
2286 | 
2287 |       // Move to the token passed the comma.
2288 |       skipEOLs();
2289 |       token = next();
2290 |     } while (true);
2291 | 
2292 |     return fieldTypeList;
2293 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.SiblingNodeIterable.iterator()

Original range: 1275-1289
Documentation:

Method code:
1275 |     @Override
1276 |     public Iterator<Node> iterator() {
1277 |       if (!used) {
1278 |         used = true;
1279 |         return this;
1280 |       } else {
1281 |         // We have already used the current object as an iterator;
